<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> And I will give you pastors according to my heart, which shall feed you with knowledge and understanding.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> And it shall come to pass, when you2f be multiplied and increased in the land, in those days, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), they shall say no more, The Ark of the Covenant of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): neither shall it come to mind: neither shall they remember it; neither shall they visit &#91;it&#93;; neither shall &#91;that&#93; be done any more.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> At that time they shall call Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; the throne of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); and all the nations shall be gathered unto it, to the name of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;: neither shall they walk any more after the imagination of their evil heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> In those days the house of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; shall walk with the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and they shall come together out of the land of the north to the land that I have given for an inheritance unto your fathers.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> But I said, How shall I put youi among the children, and give youi a pleasant land, a goodly heritage of the hosts of nations? and I said, youi shall call me, My father; and shall not turn away from me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Surely &#91;as&#93; a wife treacherously departs from her husband, so have you2f dealt treacherously with me, O house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> A voice was heard upon the high places, weeping &#91;and&#93; supplications of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: for they have perverted their way, &#91;and&#93; they have forgotten the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Return, you2f backsliding children, &#91;and&#93; I will heal your backslidings. Behold, we come unto youi; for youi &#91;are&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Truly in vain &#91;is salvation hoped for&#93; from the hills, &#91;and from&#93; the multitude of mountains: truly in the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; the salvation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> For shame has devoured the labour of our fathers from our youth; their flocks and their herds, their sons and their daughters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> We lie down in our shame, and our confusion covers us: for we have sinned against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, we and our fathers, from our youth even unto this day, and have not obeyed the voice of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> If youi will return, O Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), return unto me: and if youi will put away youri abominations out of my sight, then shall youi not remove.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> And youi shall swear, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) lives, in truth, in judgment, and in righteousness; and the nations shall bless themselves in him, and in him shall they glory.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> For thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) to the men of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, Break up your fallow (to approve, commend, accept) ground, and sow not among thorns.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> Circumcise yourselves to the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and take away the foreskins of your heart, you2f men of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;: lest my fury come forth like fire, and burn that none can quench &#91;it&#93;, because of the evil of your doings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> Declare you2f in Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and publish in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;; and say, Blow you2f the trumpet in the land: cry, gather together, and say, Assemble yourselves, and let us go into the fortified cities.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> Set up the Flag (banner) toward Tziyon &#91;parched place&#93;: retire, stay not: for I will bring evil from the north, and a great destruction.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> The lion is come up from his thicket, and the destroyer of the Gentiles is on his way; he is gone forth from his place to make youri land desolate; &#91;and&#93; youri cities shall be laid waste, without an inhabitant.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> For this gird you with sackcloth, lament and howl: for the fierce anger of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) is not turned back from us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> And it shall come to pass at that day, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), &#91;that&#93; the heart of the king shall perish, and the heart of the princes; and the priests shall be astonished, and the prophets shall wonder.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> Then said I, Ah, Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER)! surely youi have greatly deceived this people and Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, saying, You2f shall have peace; whereas the sword reaches unto the soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> At that time shall it be said to this people and to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, A dry wind of the high places in the wilderness toward the daughter of my people, not to fan (a fork-shaped tool to throw grain in the air, to let he wind separate the wheat from the chaff), nor to cleanse,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> &#91;Even&#93; a full wind from those &#91;places&#93; shall come unto me: now also will I give sentence against them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> Behold, he shall come up as clouds, and his chariots &#91;shall be&#93; as a whirlwind: his horses are swifter than eagles. Woe unto us! for we are spoiled.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> O Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, wash youri heart from wickedness, that youi may be saved. How long shall youi vain thoughts lodge within youi?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> For a voice declares from Dan &#91;Judge&#93;, and proclaims affliction from mount Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> Make you2f mention to the nations; behold, publish against Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, &#91;that&#93; watchers come from a far country, and give out their voice against the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> As keepers of a field, are they against her round about; because she has been rebellious against me, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> Your1s way and youri doings have procured these &#91;things&#93; unto youi; this &#91;is&#93; youri wickedness, because it is bitter, because it reaches unto youi heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> My bowels (inward parts; affections), my bowels (inward parts; affections)! I am pained at my very heart; my heart makes a noise in me; I cannot hold my peace, because youi have heard, O my soul, the sound of the trumpet, the alarm of war.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Destruction upon destruction is cried; for the whole land is spoiled: suddenly are my tents spoiled, &#91;and&#93; my curtains in a moment.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> How long shall I see the Flag (banner), &#91;and&#93; hear the sound of the trumpet?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> For my people &#91;is&#93; foolish, they have not known me; they &#91;are&#93; drunken children, and they have none understanding: they &#91;are&#93; wise to do evil, but to do good they have no knowledge.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> I beheld the earth, and, lo, &#91;it was&#93; without form, and void; and the heavens, and they &#91;had&#93; no light.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> I beheld the mountains, and, lo, they trembled, and all the hills moved lightly.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> I beheld, and, lo, &#91;there was&#93; no man, and all the birds of the heavens were fled.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> I beheld, and, lo, the fruitful place &#91;was&#93; a wilderness, and all the cities thereof were broken down at the presence of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), &#91;and&#93; by his fierce anger.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> For thus has the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said, The whole land shall be desolate; yet will I not make a full end.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> For this shall the earth mourn, and the heavens above be black: because I have spoken &#91;it&#93;, I have purposed &#91;it&#93;, and will not repent, neither will I turn back from it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> The whole city shall flee for the noise of the horsemen and bowmen; they shall go into thickets, and climb up upon the rocks: every city &#91;shall be&#93; forsaken, and not a man dwell in it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And &#91;when&#93; youi &#91;are&#93; spoiled, what will youi do? Though youi clothe yourself with crimson, though youi deck yourselfi with ornaments of gold, though youi rent youri face with painting, in vain shall youi make yourself fair; &#91;youri&#93; lovers will despise youi, they will seek youri life.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> For I have heard a voice as of a woman in travail, &#91;and&#93; the anguish as of her that brings forth her first child, the voice of the daughter of Tziyon &#91;parched place&#93;, &#91;that&#93; bewails herself, &#91;that&#93; spreads her hands, &#91;saying&#93;, Woe &#91;is&#93; me now! for my soul is wearied because of murderers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> Run you2f to and fro through the streets of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and see now, and know, and seek in the broad places thereof, if you2f can find a man, if there be &#91;any&#93; that executes judgment, that seeks the truth; and I will pardon it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> And though they say, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) lives; surely they swear falsely.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), &#91;are&#93; not youi eyes upon the truth? youi have stricken them, but they have not grieved; youi have consumed them, &#91;but&#93; they have refused to receive correction: they have made their faces harder than a rock; they have refused to return.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> Therefore I said, Surely these &#91;are&#93; poor; they are foolish: for they know not the way of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), &#91;nor&#93; the judgment of their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> I will get me unto the great men, and will speak unto them; for they have known the way of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), &#91;and&#93; the judgment of their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: but these have altogether broken the yoke, &#91;and&#93; burst the bonds.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> For this reason a lion out of the forest shall slay them, &#91;and&#93; a wolf of the evenings shall spoil (plunder; prey; booty) them, a leopard shall watch over their cities: everyone that goes out there shall be torn in pieces: because their transgressions are many, &#91;and&#93; their backslidings are increased.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> How shall I pardon youi for this? youri children have forsaken me, and sworn by &#91;them that are&#93; no gods: when I had fed them to the full, they then committed adultery, and assembled themselves by troops in the prostitutes’ houses.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> They were &#91;as&#93; fed horses in the morning: everyone neighed after his neighbour’s wife.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> Shall I not visit for these &#91;things&#93;? says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): and shall not my soul be avenged on such a nation as this?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> Go you2f up upon her walls, and destroy; but make not a full end: take away her battlements; for they &#91;are&#93; not the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> For the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and the house of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; have dealt very treacherously against me, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> They have belied (lied against; spoken falsely) the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and said, &#91;It is&#93; not he; neither shall evil come upon us; neither shall we see sword nor famine:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> And the prophets shall become wind, and the word &#91;is&#93; not in them: thus shall it be done unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> For what reason thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, Because you2f speak this word, behold, I will make my words in youri mouth fire, and this people wood, and it shall devour them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> Lo, I will bring a nation upon you from far, O house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): it &#91;is&#93; a mighty nation, it &#91;is&#93; an ancient nation, a nation whose language youi know not, neither understand what they say.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> Their quiver &#91;is&#93; as an open sepulchre, they &#91;are&#93; all mighty men.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> And they shall eat up youri harvest, and youri bread, &#91;which&#93; youri sons and youri daughters should eat: they shall eat up youri flocks and youri herds: they shall eat up youri vines and youri fig trees: they shall impoverish youri fenced cities, wherein youi trusted, with the sword.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> Nevertheless in those days, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), I will not make a full end with you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> And it shall come to pass, when you2f shall say, For what reason does the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; all these &#91;things&#93; unto us? then shall youi answer them, Like as you2f have forsaken me, and served strange gods in your land, so shall you2f serve strangers in a land &#91;that is&#93; not yours.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Declare this in the house of Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;, and publish it in Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Hear now this, O foolish people, and without understanding; which have eyes, and see not; which have ears, and hear not:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Fear you2f not me? says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): will you2f not tremble at my presence, which have placed the sand &#91;for&#93; the bound of the sea by a perpetual decree, that it cannot pass it: and though the waves thereof toss themselves, yet can they not prevail; though they roar, yet can they not pass over it?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> But this people has a revolting and a rebellious heart; they are revolted and gone.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Neither say they in their heart, Let us now fear the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, that gives rain, both the former and the latter, in his season: he reserves unto us the appointed weeks of the harvest.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> Your iniquities have turned away these &#91;things&#93;, and your sins have withheld good &#91;things&#93; from you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> For among my people are found wicked &#91;men&#93;: they lay wait, as he that sets snares; they set a trap, they catch men.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> As a cage is full of birds, so &#91;are&#93; their houses full of deceit: therefore they are become great, and waxen rich.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> They are waxen fat, they shine: yes, they overpass the deeds of the wicked: they judge not the cause, the cause of the fatherless, yet they prosper; and the right of the needy do they not judge.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Shall I not visit for these &#91;things&#93;? says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): shall not my soul be avenged on such a nation as this?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> A wonderful and horrible thing is committed in the land;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> The prophets prophesy falsely, and the priests bear rule by their means; and my people love &#91;to have it&#93; so: and what will you2f do in the end thereof?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> O you2f children of Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93;, gather yourselves to flee out of the midst of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and blow the trumpet in T’koa &#91;a stockade&#93;, and set up a sign of fire in Beit-Hakerem &#91;house of the vineyard&#93;: for evil appears out of the north, and great destruction.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> I have likened the daughter of Tziyon &#91;parched place&#93; to a comely (attractive; proper (one’s own; peculiar; particular); becoming) and delicate &#91;woman&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">

But they said, We will not walk &#91;in it&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> Therefore hear, you2f nations, and know, O congregation, what &#91;is&#93; among them.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> Hear, O earth: behold, I will bring evil upon this people, &#91;even&#93; the fruit of their thoughts, because they have not listened unto my words, nor to my law, but rejected it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), Behold, I will lay stumblingblocks before this people, and the fathers and the sons together shall fall upon them; the neighbour and his friend shall perish.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), Behold, a people comes from the north country, and a great nation shall be raised from the sides of the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> They shall lay hold on bow and spear; they &#91;are&#93; cruel, and have no mercy; their voice roars like the sea; and they ride upon horses, set in array (to put on; to clothe) as men for war against youi, O daughter of Tziyon &#91;parched place&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> O daughter of my people, gird &#91;youi&#93; with sackcloth, and wallow (to approve, commend, accept) yourself in ashes: make youri mourning, &#91;as for&#93; an only son, most bitter lamentation: for the spoiler shall suddenly come upon us.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, my anger and my fury shall be poured out upon this place, upon man, and upon beast, and upon the trees of the field, and upon the fruit of the ground; and it shall burn, and shall not be quenched.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Put your burnt offerings unto your sacrifices, and eat flesh.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> But this thing commanded I them, saying, Obey my voice, and I will be your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and you2f shall be my people: and walk you2f in all the ways that I have commanded you, that it may be well unto you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> But youi shall say unto them, This &#91;is&#93; a nation that obeys not the voice of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, nor receives correction: tis perished, and is cut off from their mouth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Cut off youi hair, &#91;O Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;&#93;, and cast &#91;it&#93; away, and take up a lamentation on high places; for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has rejected and forsaken the generation of his wrath.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> For the children of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; have done evil in my sight, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): they have set their abominations in the house which is called by my name, to pollute it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Therefore, behold, the days come, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that it shall no more be called Tofet &#91;place of fire&#93;, nor the valley of the son of Hinnom &#91;Lamentation&#93;, but the valley of slaughter: for they shall bury in Tofet &#91;place of fire&#93;, till there be no place.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And the carcasses of this people shall be meat for the fowls of the heaven, and for the beasts of the earth; and none shall frighten (make afriad) &#91;them&#93; away.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> Then will I cause to cease from the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and from the streets of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, the voice of gladness, and the voice of gladness, the voice of the bridegroom, and the voice of the bride: for the land shall be desolate.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> Why do we sit still? assemble yourselves, and let us enter into the fortified cities, and let us be silent there: for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; has put us to silence, and given us water of gall (something bitter or poisonous) to (to do entirely, do wholly) drink, because we have sinned against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> We looked for peace, but no good &#91;came; and&#93; for a time of health, and behold trouble!</span>

<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> The snorting of his horses was heard from Dan &#91;Judge&#93;: the whole land trembled at the sound of the neighing of his strong ones; for they are come, and have devoured the land, and all that is in it; the city, and those that dwell in it.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> Behold the voice of the cry of the daughter of my people because of them that dwell in a far country: &#91;Is&#93; not the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in Tziyon &#91;parched place&#93;? &#91;is&#93; not her king in her? Why have they provoked me to anger with their graven images, &#91;and&#93; with strange vanities?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> The harvest is past, the summer is ended, and we are not saved.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> For the hurt of the daughter of my people am I hurt; I am black; astonishment has taken hold on me.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> &#91;Is there&#93; no balm (a medicine; rosin from a bush) in Gil’ad &#91;Rocky Region&#93;; &#91;is there&#93; no physician there? why then is not the health of the daughter of my people recovered?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> Oh that my head were waters, and my eyes a fountain of tears, that I might weep day and night for the slain of the daughter of my people!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Oh that I had in the wilderness a lodging place of wayfaring men; that I might leave my people, and go from them! for they &#91;be&#93; all adulterers, an assembly of treacherous men.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> And they bend their tongues &#91;like&#93; their bow &#91;for&#93; lies: but they are not valiant for the tupon the earth; for they proceed from evil to evil, and they know not me, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> Take you2f heed everyone of his neighbour, and trust you2f not in any brother: for every brother will utterly supplant, and every neighbour will walk with slanders.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> And they will deceive everyone his neighbour, and will not speak the truth: they have taught their tongue to speak lies, &#91;and&#93; weary themselves to commit iniquity.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> You1s habitation &#91;is&#93; in the midst of deceit; through deceit they refuse to know me, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, Behold, I will melt them, and try them; for how shall I do for the daughter of my people?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> Their tongue &#91;is as&#93; an arrow shot out; it speaks deceit: &#91;one&#93; speaks peaceably to his neighbour with his mouth, but in heart he lays his wait.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> Shall I not visit them for these &#91;things&#93;? says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): shall not my soul be avenged on such a nation as this?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> For the mountains will I take up a weeping and wailing, and for the habitations of the wilderness a lamentation, because they are burned up, so that none can pass through &#91;them&#93;; neither can &#91;men&#93; hear the voice of the cattle; both the fowl of the heavens and the beast are fled; they are gone.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> Who &#91;is&#93; the wise man, that may understand this? and &#91;who is he&#93; to whom the mouth of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has spoken, that he may declare it, for what the land perishes &#91;and&#93; is burned up like a wilderness, that none passes through?</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Behold, I will feed them, &#91;even&#93; this people, with wormwood, and give them water of gall (something bitter or poisonous) to (to do entirely, do wholly) drink.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, Consider you2f, and call for the mourning women, that they may come; and send for cunning &#91;women&#93;, that they may come:</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> And let them make haste, and take up a wailing for us, that our eyes may run down with tears, and our eyelids gush out with waters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> For a voice of wailing is heard out of Tziyon &#91;parched place&#93;, How are we spoiled! we are greatly confounded, because we have forsaken the land, because our dwellings have cast &#91;us&#93; out.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Yet hear the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), O you2f women, and let your ear receive the word of his mouth, and teach your daughters wailing, and everyone her neighbour lamentation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> For death is come up into our windows, &#91;and&#93; is entered into our palaces, to cut off the children from without, &#91;and&#93; the young men from the streets.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Speak, Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), Even the carcasses of men shall fall as dung upon the open field, and as the handful after the harvestman, and none shall gather &#91;them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), Let not the wise &#91;man&#93; glory in his wisdom, neither let the mighty &#91;man&#93; glory in his might, let not the rich &#91;man&#93; glory in his riches:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> But let him that glories glory in this, that he understands and knows me, that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) which exercise lovingkindness, judgment, and righteousness, in the earth: for in these &#91;things&#93; I delight, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> Behold, the days come, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that I will punish all &#91;them which are&#93; circumcised with the uncircumcised;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and Edom &#91;Red&#93;, and the children of ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93;, and Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, and all &#91;that are&#93; in the utmost corners, that dwell in the wilderness: for all &#91;these&#93; nations &#91;are&#93; uncircumcised, and all the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; &#91;are&#93; uncircumcised in the heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> Hear you2f the word which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) speaks unto you, O house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;:</span>
</div>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> Who would not fear youi, O King of nations? for to youi does it appertain (to belong to; to pertain to): forasmuch as among all the wise &#91;men&#93; of the nations, and in all their kingdoms, &#91;there is&#93; none like unto youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> But they are altogether cruel and foolish: the stock &#91;is&#93; a doctrine of vanities.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> Thus shall you2f say unto them, The gods that have not made the heavens and the earth, &#91;even&#93; they shall perish from the earth, and from under these heavens.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> He has made the earth by his power, he has established the world by his wisdom, and has stretched out the heavens by his discretion.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> When he utters his voice, &#91;there is&#93; a multitude of waters in the heavens, and he causes the vapours to ascend from the ends of the earth; he makes lightnings with rain, and brings forth the wind out of his treasures.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> For thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), Behold, I will sling out the inhabitants of the land at this once, and will distress them, that they may find &#91;it so&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> My tabernacle is spoiled, and all my cords are broken: my children are gone forth of me, and they &#91;are&#93; not: &#91;there is&#93; none to stretch forth my tent any more, and to set up my curtains.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> For the pastors are become cruel, and have not sought the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): therefore they shall not prosper, and all their flocks shall be scattered.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Behold, the noise of the report is come, and a great commotion out of the north country, to make the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; desolate, &#91;and&#93; a den of dragons.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), I know that the way of man &#91;is&#93; not in himself: &#91;it is&#93; not in man that walks to direct his steps.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), correct me, but with judgment; not in youri anger, lest youi bring me to nothing.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> Pour out youri fury upon the heathen that know youi not, and upon the families that call not on youri name: for they have eaten up Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;, and devoured him, and consumed him, and have made his habitation desolate.</span>
</div>

</div>

<div className="bp">

</div>

Then answered I, and said, So be it, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> Then the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto me, Proclaim all these words in the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and in the streets of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, saying, Hear you2f the words of this covenant, and do them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> For I earnestly protested unto your fathers in the day &#91;that&#93; I brought them up out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, &#91;even&#93; unto this day, rising early and protesting, saying, Obey my voice.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> Yet they obeyed not, nor inclined their ear, but walked everyone in the imagination of their evil heart: therefore I will bring upon them all the words of this covenant, which I commanded &#91;them&#93; to do; but they did &#91;them&#93; not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto me, A conspiracy is found among the men of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and among the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> They are turned back to the iniquities of their forefathers, which refused to hear my words; and they went after other gods to serve them: the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and the house of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; have broken my covenant which I made with their fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), Behold, I will bring evil upon them, which they shall not be able to escape; and though they shall cry unto me, I will not hearken unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> Then shall the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; go, and cry unto the gods unto whom they offer incense: but they shall not save them at all in the time of their trouble.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> For &#91;according to&#93; the number of youri cities were youri gods, O Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;; and &#91;according to&#93; the number of the streets of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; have you2f set up altars to &#91;that&#93; shameful thing, &#91;even&#93; altars to burn incense unto Ba’al &#91;lord (a false god)&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> Therefore pray not youi for this people, neither lift up a cry or prayer for them: for I will not hear &#91;them&#93; in the time that they cry unto me for their trouble.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> What has my beloved to do in my house, &#91;seeing&#93; she has wrought lewdness with many, and the holy flesh is passed from youi? when youi do evil, then youi rejoice.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) called youi name, A green olive tree, fair, &#91;and&#93; of goodly fruit: with the noise of a great tumult he has kindled fire upon it, and the branches of it are broken.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> For the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, that planted youi, has pronounced evil against youi, for the evil of the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and of the house of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, which they have done against themselves to provoke me to anger in offering incense unto Ba’al &#91;lord (a false god)&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has given me knowledge &#91;of it&#93;, and I know &#91;it&#93;: then youi showed me their doings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> But I &#91;was&#93; like a lamb &#91;or&#93; an ox &#91;that&#93; is brought to the slaughter; and I knew not that they had devised devices against me, &#91;saying&#93;, Let us destroy the tree with the fruit thereof, and let us cut him off from the land of the living, that his name may be no more remembered.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> But, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, that judge righteously, that try the reins (literally, kidneys; figuratively, seat of emotion) and the heart, let me see youri vengeance on them: for unto youi have I revealed my cause.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) of the men of ‘Anatot &#91;answers to prayer&#93;, that seek youri life, saying, Prophesy not in the name of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that youi die not by our hand:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, Behold, I will punish them: the young men shall die by the sword; their sons and their daughters shall die by famine:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And there shall be no remnant of them: for I will bring evil upon the men of ‘Anatot &#91;answers to prayer&#93;, &#91;even&#93; the year of their visitation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> Righteous &#91;are&#93; youi, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), when I plead with youi: yet let me talk with youi of &#91;youri&#93; judgments: For what reason does the way of the wicked prosper? &#91;wherefore&#93; are all they happy that deal very treacherously?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Youi have planted them, yes, they have taken root: they grow, yes, they bring forth fruit: youi &#91;are&#93; near in their mouth, and far from their reins (literally, kidneys; figuratively, seat of emotion).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> But youi, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), know me: youi have seen me, and tried my heart toward youi: pull them out like sheep for the slaughter, and prepare them for the day of slaughter.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> How long shall the land mourn, and the herbs of every field wither, for the wickedness of them that dwell in it? the beasts are consumed, and the birds; because they said, He shall not see our last end.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> If youi have run with the footmen, and they have wearied youi, then how can youi contend with horses? and &#91;if&#93; in the land of peace, &#91;wherein&#93; youi trusted, &#91;they wearied youi&#93;, then how will youi do in the swelling of Yarden &#91;Descender&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> For even youri brethren, and the house of youri father, even they have dealt treacherously with youri; yes, they have called a multitude after youi: believe them not, though they speak fair words unto youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> I have forsaken my house, I have left my heritage; I have given the dearly beloved of my soul into the hand of her enemies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> Mine heritage is unto me as a lion in the forest; it cries out against me: therefore have I hated it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> Mine heritage &#91;is&#93; unto me &#91;as&#93; a speckled bird, the birds round about &#91;are&#93; against her; come you2f, assemble all the beasts of the field, come to devour.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> Many pastors have destroyed my vineyard, they have trampled my portion under foot, they have made my pleasant portion a desolate wilderness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> They have made it desolate, &#91;and being&#93; desolate it mourns unto me; the whole land is made desolate, because no man lays &#91;it&#93; to heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> The spoilers are come upon all high places through the wilderness: for the sword of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) shall devour from the &#91;one&#93; end of the land even to the &#91;other&#93; end of the land: no flesh shall have peace.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> They have sown wheat, but shall reap thorns: they have put themselves to pain, &#91;but&#93; shall not profit: and they shall be ashamed of your revenues because of the fierce anger of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) against all my evil neighbours, that touch the inheritance which I have caused my people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; to inherit; Behold, I will pluck them out of their land, and pluck out the house of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; from among them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> And it shall come to pass, after that I have plucked them out I will return, and have compassion on them, and will bring them again, every man to his heritage, and every man to his land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> And it shall come to pass, if they will diligently learn the ways of my people, to swear by my name, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) lives; as they taught my people to swear by Ba’al &#91;lord (a false god)&#93;; then shall they be built in the midst of my people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> But if they will not obey, I will utterly pluck up and destroy that nation, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto me, Go and get youi a linen belt, and put it upon youi waist, and put it not in water.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> So I got a belt according to the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and put &#91;it&#93; on my waist.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> And the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me the second time, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> Take the belt that youi have got, which &#91;is&#93; upon youri waist, and arise, go to Euphrates (to break forth, to gush), and hide it there in a hole of the rock.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> So I went, and hid it by Euphrates (to break forth, to gush), as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> And it came to pass after many days, that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto me, Arise, go to Euphrates (to break forth, to gush), and take the belt from there, which I commanded youi to hide there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> Then I went to Euphrates (to break forth, to gush), and excavated, and took the belt from the place where I had hid it: and, behold, the belt was marred, it was profitable for nothing.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> Then the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), After this manner will I mar the pride of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and the great pride of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> This evil people, which refuse to hear my words, which walk in the imagination of their heart, and walk after other gods, to serve them, and to worship them, shall even be as this belt, which is good for nothing.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> For as the belt cleaves to the waist of a man, so have I caused to cling to (hold onto; stick to) me the whole house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and the whole house of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); that they might be unto me for a people, and for a name, and for a praise, and for a glory: but they would not hear.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> Therefore youi shall speak unto them this word; Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, Every bottle shall be filled with wine: and they shall say unto youi, Do we not certainly know that every bottle shall be filled with wine?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> Then shall youi say unto them, Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), Behold, I will fill all the inhabitants of this land, even the kings that sit upon David’s &#91;beloved&#93;’s throne, and the priests, and the prophets, and all the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, with drunkenness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> And I will dash them one against another, even the fathers and the sons together, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): I will not pity, nor spare, nor have mercy, but destroy them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> Hear you2f, and give ear; be not proud: for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has spoken.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> Give glory to the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, before he cause darkness, and before your feet stumble upon the dark mountains, and, while you2f look for light, he turn it into the shadow of death, &#91;and&#93; make &#91;it&#93; gross darkness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> But if you2f will not hear it, my soul shall weep in secret places for &#91;your&#93; pride; and my eye shall weep sore, and run down with tears, because the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; flock is carried away captive.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> Say unto the king and to the queen, Humble yourselves, sit down: for your Principalities (rulers or their territory) shall come down, &#91;even&#93; the crown of your glory.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> The cities of the south shall be shut up, and none shall open &#91;them&#93;: Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; shall be carried away captive all of it, it shall be wholly carried away captive.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Lift up your eyes, and behold them that come from the north: where &#91;is&#93; the flock &#91;that&#93; was given youi, youri beautiful flock?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> What will youi say when he shall punish youi? for youi have taught them &#91;to be&#93; captains, &#91;and&#93; as chief over youi: shall not sorrows take youi, as a woman in travail?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> And if youi say in youri heart, For what reason come these things upon me? For the greatness of youi iniquity are youi skirts discovered, &#91;and&#93; youi heels made bore.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Can the Cushi (Ethiopian) (Kushi &#91;their blackness&#93;) change his skin, or the leopard his spots? &#91;then&#93; may you2f also do good, that are accustomed to do evil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Therefore will I scatter them as the stubble that passes away by the wind of the wilderness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> This &#91;is&#93; youri lot, the portion of youri measures from me, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); because youi have forgotten me, and trusted in falsehood.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Therefore will I discover (uncover; reveal; have first sight of) youri skirts upon youri face, that youri shame may appear.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> I have seen youri adulteries, and youri neighings, the lewdness of youri whoredom, &#91;and&#93; youi abominations on the hills in the fields. Woe unto youi, O Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;! will youi not be made clean? when &#91;shall it&#93; once &#91;be&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> The word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) that came to Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; concerning the famine.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; mourns, and the gates thereof languish; they are black unto the ground; and the cry of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; is gone up.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> And their nobles have sent their little ones to the waters: they came to the pits, &#91;and&#93; found no water; they returned with their vessels empty; they were ashamed and confounded, and covered their heads.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> Because the ground is chapped, for there was no rain in the earth, the plowmen were ashamed, they covered their heads.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> Yes, the hind also calved in the field, and forsook &#91;it&#93;, because there was no grass.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> And the wild asses did stand in the high places, they snuffed up the wind like dragons; their eyes did fail, because &#91;there was&#93; no grass.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), though our iniquities testify against us, do youi &#91;it&#93; for youi name’s sake: for our backslidings are many; we have sinned against youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> O the hope of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, the saviour thereof in time of trouble, why should youi be as a stranger in the land, and as a wayfaring man &#91;that&#93; turns aside to wait for a night?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> Why should youi be as a man astonished, as a mighty man &#91;that&#93; cannot save? yet youi, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), &#91;are&#93; in the midst of us, and we are called by youi name; leave us not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto this people, Thus have they loved to wander, they have not refrained their feet, therefore the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) does not accept them; he will now remember their iniquity, and visit their sins.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> Then said the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto me, Pray not for this people for &#91;their&#93; good.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> When they fast, I will not hear their cry; and when they offer burnt offering and an oblation, I will not accept them: but I will consume them by the sword, and by the famine, and by the pestilence.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> Then said I, Ah, Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER)! behold, the prophets say unto them, You2f shall not see the sword, neither shall you2f have famine; but I will give you assured peace in this place.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> Then the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto me, The prophets prophesy lies in my name: I sent them not, neither have I commanded them, neither spoke unto them: they prophesy unto you a false vision and divination, and a thing of nothing, and the deceit of their heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) concerning the prophets that prophesy in my name, and I sent them not, yet they say, Sword and famine shall not be in this land; By sword and famine shall those prophets be consumed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> And the people to whom they prophesy shall be cast out in the streets of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; because of the famine and the sword; and they shall have none to bury them, them, their wives, nor their sons, nor their daughters: for I will pour their wickedness upon them.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> If I go forth into the field, then behold the slain with the sword! and if I enter into the city, then behold them that are sick with famine! yes, both the prophet and the priest go about into a land that they know not.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Do not abhor &#91;us&#93;, for youri name’s sake, do not disgrace the throne of youri glory: remember, break not youri covenant with us.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Are there &#91;any&#93; among the vanities of the Gentiles that can cause rain? or can the heavens give showers? &#91;are&#93; not youi he, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;? therefore we will wait upon youi: for youi have made all these &#91;things&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> Then said the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto me, Though Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Sh’mu’el &#91;His name is El&#93; stood before me, &#91;yet&#93; my mind &#91;could&#93; not &#91;be&#93; toward this people: cast &#91;them&#93; out of my sight, and let them go forth.</span>

I have neither lent on usury (interest paid for using money), nor men have lent to me on usury (interest paid for using money); &#91;yet&#93; everyone of them does curse me.</span>

<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> And I will make &#91;youi&#93; to pass with youri enemies into a land &#91;which&#93; youi know not: for a fire is kindled in my anger, &#91;which&#93; shall burn upon you.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), youi know: remember me, and visit me, and revenge me of my persecutors; take me not away in youri longsuffering: know that for youi sake I have suffered rebuke (reprimand; strongly warn; restrain).</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> For thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Behold, I will cause to cease out of this place in your eyes, and in your days, the voice of gladness, and the voice of gladness, the voice of the bridegroom, and the voice of the bride.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) search the heart, &#91;I&#93; try the eins (literally, kidneys; figuratively, seat of emotion), even to give every man according to his ways, &#91;and&#93; according to the fruit of his doings.</span>

<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> Thus said the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto me; Go and stand in the gate of the children of the people, whereby the kings of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; come in, and by the which they go out, and in all the gates of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;;</span>
