</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Enquire, I pray youi, of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) for us; for N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; makes war against us; if so be that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) will deal with us according to all his wondrous works, that he may go up from us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> Then said Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; unto them, Thus shall you2f say to Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Behold, I will turn back the weapons of war that &#91;are&#93; in your hands, which you2f fight against the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and &#91;against&#93; the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;, which besiege you without the walls, and I will assemble them into the midst of this city.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> And I myself will fight against you with an outstretched hand and with a strong arm, even in anger, and in fury, and in great wrath.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> And I will strike the inhabitants of this city, both man and beast: they shall die of a great pestilence.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> And afterward, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), I will deliver Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and his servants, and the people, and such as are left in this city from the pestilence, from the sword, and from the famine, into the hand of N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and into the hand of their enemies, and into the hand of those that seek their life: and he shall strike them with the edge of the sword; he shall not spare them, neither have pity, nor have mercy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> And unto this people youi shall say, Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Behold, I set before you the way of life, and the way of death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> He that abides in this city shall die by the sword, and by the famine, and by the pestilence: but he that goes out, and falls to the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93; that besiege you, he shall live, and his life shall be unto him for a prey.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> For I have set my face against this city for evil, and not for good, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): it shall be given into the hand of the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and he shall burn it with fire.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> And touching the house of the king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, &#91;say&#93;, Hear you2f the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate);</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> O house of David &#91;beloved&#93;, thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Execute judgment in the morning, and deliver &#91;him that is&#93; spoiled out of the hand of the oppressor, lest my fury go out like fire, and burn that none can quench &#91;it&#93;, because of the evil of your doings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> Behold, I &#91;am&#93; against youi, O inhabitant of the valley, &#91;and&#93; rock of the plain, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); which say, Who shall come down against us? or who shall enter into our habitations?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> But I will punish you according to the fruit of your doings, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): and I will kindle a fire in the forest thereof, and it shall devour all things round about it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Go down to the house of the king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and speak there this word,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> And say, Hear the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), O king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, that sit upon the throne of David &#91;beloved&#93;, youi, and youi servants, and youi people that enter in by these gates:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Execute you2f judgment and righteousness, and deliver the spoiled out of the hand of the oppressor: and do no wrong, do no violence to the stranger, the fatherless, nor the widow, neither shed innocent blood in this place.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> For if you2f do this thing indeed, then shall there enter in by the gates of this house kings sitting upon the throne of David &#91;beloved&#93;, riding in chariots and on horses, he, and his servants, and his people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> But if you2f will not hear these words, I swear by myself, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that this house shall become a desolation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> For thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto the king’s house of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;; youi &#91;are&#93; Gil’ad &#91;Rocky Region&#93; unto me, &#91;and&#93; the head of L’vanon &#91;Whiteness&#93;: &#91;yet&#93; surely I will make youi a wilderness, &#91;and&#93; cities &#91;which&#93; are not inhabited.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> And I will prepare destroyers against youi, everyone with his weapons: and they shall cut down youri choice cedars, and cast &#91;them&#93; into the fire.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> And many nations shall pass by this city, and they shall say every man to his neighbour, For what reason has the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) done thus unto this great city?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> Then they shall answer, Because they have forsaken the covenant of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and worshipped other gods, and served them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> Weep you2f not for the dead, neither bemoan him: &#91;but&#93; weep sore for him that goes away: for he shall return no more, nor see his native country.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> For thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) touching Shalum &#91;retribution&#93; the son of Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, which reigned instead of Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; his father, which went forth out of this place; He shall not return there any more:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> But he shall die in the place to which they have led him captive, and shall see this land no more.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> Woe unto him that builds his house by unrighteousness, and his chambers by wrong; &#91;that&#93; uses his neighbour’s service without wages, and gives him not for his work;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> That says, I will build me a wide house and large chambers, and cuts him out windows; and &#91;it’s&#93; cieling with cedar, and painted with vermilion.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> Shalt youi reign, because youi clothe &#91;yourself&#93; in cedar? did not youri father eat and drink, and do judgment and justice, &#91;and&#93; then &#91;it was&#93; well with him?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> He judged the cause of the poor and needy; then &#91;it was&#93; well &#91;with him: was&#93; not this to know me? says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> But youri eyes and youri heart &#91;are&#93; not but for youri covetousness, and for to shed innocent blood, and for oppression, and for violence, to do &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) concerning Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93; the son of Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;; They shall not lament for him, &#91;saying&#93;, Ah my brother! or, Ah sister! they shall not lament for him, &#91;saying&#93;, Ah lord! or, Ah his glory!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> He shall be buried with the burial of an ass, drawn and cast forth beyond the gates of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Go up to L’vanon &#91;Whiteness&#93;, and cry; and lift up youri voice in Bashan &#91;Fruitful&#93;, and cry from the passages: for all youri lovers are destroyed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> I spoke unto youi in youri prosperity; &#91;but&#93; youi said, I will not hear. This &#91;has been&#93; youri manner from youri youth, that youi obeyed not my voice.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> The wind shall eat up all youri pastors, and youri lovers shall go into captivity: surely then shall youi be ashamed and confounded for all youri wickedness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> O inhabitant of L’vanon &#91;Whiteness&#93;, that makes youri nest in the cedars, how gracious shall youi be when pangs (extreme sharp pains) come upon youi, the pain as of a woman in travail!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> &#91;As&#93; I live, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), though Koniyahu &#91;Yehovah will establish&#93; the son of Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; were the signet (seal or stamp) upon my right hand, yet would I pluck youi there;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And I will give youi into the hand of them that seek youri life, and into the hand &#91;of them&#93; whose face youi fear, even into the hand of N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and into the hand of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> And I will cast youi out, and youri mother that bore youi, into another country, where you2f were not born; and there shall you2f die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> But to the land whereunto they desire to return, there shall they not return.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> &#91;Is&#93; this man Koniyahu &#91;Yehovah will establish&#93; a despised broken idol? &#91;is he&#93; a vessel wherein &#91;is&#93; no pleasure? therefore are they cast out, he and his seed, and are cast into a land which they know not?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> O earth, earth, earth, hear the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), Write you2f this man childless, a man &#91;that&#93; shall not prosper in his days: for no man of his seed shall prosper, sitting upon the throne of David &#91;beloved&#93;, and ruling any more in Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> Woe be unto the pastors that destroy and scatter the sheep of my pasture! says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; against the pastors that feed my people; You2f have scattered my flock, and driven them away, and have not visited them: behold, I will visit upon you the evil of your doings, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> And I will gather the remnant of my flock out of all countries to which I have driven them, and will bring them again to their folds; and they shall be fruitful and increase.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> And I will set up shepherds over them which shall feed them: and they shall fear no more, nor be dismayed, neither shall they be lacking, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> Behold, the days come, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that I will raise unto David &#91;beloved&#93; a righteous Branch, and a King shall reign and prosper, and shall execute judgment and justice in the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> In his days Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; shall be saved, and Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; shall dwell safely: and this &#91;is&#93; His Name whereby he shall be called, THE LORD-Yehōvah Tzudkenu &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate OUR RIGHTEOUSNESS&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> Therefore, behold, the days come, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that they shall no more say, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) lives, which brought up the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> But, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) lives, which brought up and which led the seed of the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; out of the north country, and from all countries to which I had driven them; and they shall dwell in their own land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> Mine heart within me is broken because of the prophets; all my bones shake; I am like a drunken man, and like a man whom wine has overcome, because of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and because of the words of his holiness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> For the land is full of adulterers; for because of swearing the land mourns; the pleasant places of the wilderness are dried up, and their course is evil, and their force &#91;is&#93; not right.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> For both prophet and priest are Profane (treat as common; defile); yes, in my house have I found their wickedness, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> For what reason their way shall be unto them as slippery &#91;ways&#93; in the darkness: they shall be driven on, and fall in it: for I will bring evil upon them, &#91;even&#93; the year of their visitation, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> And I have seen folly in the prophets of Shomron &#91;watch mountain&#93;; they prophesied in Ba’al &#91;lord (a false god)&#93;, and caused my people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; to err.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> I have seen also in the prophets of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; an horrible thing: they commit adultery, and walk in lies: they strengthen also the hands of evildoers, that none does return from his wickedness: they are all of them unto me as S’dom &#91;Burning&#93;, and the inhabitants thereof as ‘Amora &#91;Submersion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93; concerning the prophets; Behold, I will feed them with wormwood, and make them drink the water of gall (something bitter or poisonous): for from the prophets of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; is profaneness gone forth into all the land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, Listen not unto the words of the prophets that prophesy unto you: they make you vain: they speak a vision of their own heart, &#91;and&#93; not out of the mouth of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> They say still unto them that despise me, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has said, You2f shall have peace; and they say unto everyone that walks after the imagination of his own heart, No evil shall come upon you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> For who has stood in the counsel of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and has perceived and heard his word? who has marked his word, and heard &#91;it&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> Behold, a whirlwind of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) is gone forth in fury, even a grievous whirlwind: it shall fall grievously upon the head of the wicked.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> The anger of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) shall not return, until he have executed, and till he have performed the thoughts of his heart: in the latter days you2f shall consider it perfectly.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> I have not sent these prophets, yet they ran: I have not spoken to them, yet they prophesied.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> But if they had stood in my counsel, and had caused my people to hear my words, then they should have turned them from their evil way, and from the evil of their doings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> &#91;Am&#93; I a God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; at hand, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and not a God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; afar off?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Can any hide himself in secret places that I shall not see him? says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate). Do not I fill heaven and earth? says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> I have heard what the prophets said, that prophesy lies in my name, saying, I have dreamed, I have dreamed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> How long shall &#91;this&#93; be in the heart of the prophets that prophesy lies? yes, &#91;they are&#93; prophets of the deceit of their own heart;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Which think to cause my people to forget my name by their dreams which they tell every man to his neighbour, as their fathers have forgotten my name for Ba’al &#91;lord (a false god)&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> The prophet that has a dream, let him tell a dream; and he that has my word, let him speak my word faithfully. What &#91;is&#93; the chaff to the wheat? says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> &#91;Is&#93; not my word like as a fire? says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); and like a hammer &#91;that&#93; breaks the rock in pieces?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Therefore, behold, I &#91;am&#93; against the prophets, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that steal my words everyone from his neighbour.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> Behold, I &#91;am&#93; against the prophets, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that use their tongues, and say, He says.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Behold, I &#91;am&#93; against them that prophesy false dreams, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and do tell them, and cause my people to err by their lies, and by their lightness; yet I sent them not, nor commanded them: therefore they shall not profit this people at all, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And when this people, or the prophet, or a priest, shall ask youi, saying, What &#91;is&#93; the burden of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate)? youi shall then say unto them, What burden? I will even forsake you, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And &#91;as for&#93; the prophet, and the priest, and the people, that shall say, The burden of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), I will even punish that man and his house.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> Thus shall you2f say everyone to his neighbour, and everyone to his brother, What has the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) answered? and, What has the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoken?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And the burden of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) shall you2f mention no more: for every man’s word shall be his burden; for you2f have perverted the words of El Chai &#91;The Living God&#93;, of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93; our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> Thus shall youi say to the prophet, What has the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) answered youi? and, What has the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoken?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> But since you2f say, The burden of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Because you2f say this word, The burden of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and I have sent unto you, saying, You2f shall not say, The burden of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate);</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> Therefore, behold, I, even I, will utterly forget you, and I will forsake you, and the city that I gave you and your fathers, &#91;and cast you&#93; out of my presence:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> And I will bring an everlasting reproach (disgrace; shame) upon you, and a perpetual shame, which shall not be forgotten.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) showed me, and, behold, two baskets of figs &#91;were&#93; set before the temple of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), after that N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; had carried away captive Y’khanyahu &#91;Yehovah will establish&#93; the son of Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and the princes of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, with the carpenters and smiths, from Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and had brought them to Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> One basket &#91;had&#93; very good figs, &#91;even&#93; like the figs &#91;that are&#93; first ripe: and the other basket &#91;had&#93; very naughty figs, which could not be eaten, they were so bad.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> Then said the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto me, What see youi, Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;? And I said, Figs; the good figs, very good; and the evil, very evil, that cannot be eaten, they are so evil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> Again the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Like these good figs, so will I acknowledge them that are carried away captive of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, whom I have sent out of this place into the land of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93; for &#91;their&#93; good.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> For I will set my eyes upon them for good, and I will bring them again to this land: and I will build them, and not pull &#91;them&#93; down; and I will plant them, and not pluck &#91;them&#93; up.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> And I will give them an heart to know me, that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): and they shall be my people, and I will be their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: for they shall return unto me with their whole heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> And as the evil figs, which cannot be eaten, they are so evil; surely thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), So will I give Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; the king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and his princes, and the residue (part that remains, the rest) of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, that remain in this land, and them that dwell in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> And I will deliver them to be removed into all the kingdoms of the earth for &#91;their&#93; hurt, &#91;to be&#93; a reproach (disgrace; shame) and a proverb, a taunt and a curse, in all places to which I shall drive them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> And I will send the sword, the famine, and the pestilence, among them, till they be consumed from off the land that I gave unto them and to their fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> The word that came to Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; concerning all the people of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; in the fourth year of Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93; the son of Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, that &#91;was&#93; the first year of N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> From the thirteenth year of Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; the son of ‘Amon &#91;master workman&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, even unto this day, that &#91;is&#93; the three and twentieth year, the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has come unto me, and I have spoken unto you, rising early and speaking; but you2f have not listened.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has sent unto you all his servants the prophets, rising early and sending &#91;them&#93;; but you2f have not listened, nor inclined your ear to hear.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> They said, Turn you2f again now everyone from his evil way, and from the evil of your doings, and dwell in the land that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has given unto you and to your fathers forever and ever:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> And go not after other gods to serve them, and to worship them, and provoke me not to anger with the works of your hands; and I will do you no hurt.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> Yet you2f have not listened unto me, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); that you2f might provoke me to anger with the works of your hands to your own hurt.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;; Because you2f have not heard my words,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> Behold, I will send and take all the families of the north, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, my servant, and will bring them against this land, and against the inhabitants thereof, and against all these nations round about, and will utterly destroy them, and make them an astonishment, and an hissing, and perpetual desolations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> Moreover I will take from them the voice of gladness, and the voice of gladness, the voice of the bridegroom, and the voice of the bride, the sound of the millstones, and the light of the candle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> And this whole land shall be a desolation, &#91;and&#93; an astonishment; and these nations shall serve the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; seventy years.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> And it shall come to pass, when seventy years are accomplished, &#91;that&#93; I will punish the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and that nation, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), for their iniquity, and the land of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;, and will make it perpetual desolations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> And I will bring upon that land all my words which I have pronounced against it, &#91;even&#93; all that is written in this book, which Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; has prophesied against all the nations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> For many nations and great kings shall serve themselves of them also: and I will recompense them according to their deeds, and according to the works of their own hands.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> For thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; unto me; Take the wine cup of this fury at my hand, and cause all the nations, to whom I send youi, to drink it.</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> And they shall drink, and be moved, and be mad, because of the sword that I will send among them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And all the kings of Zimri &#91;My Music&#93;, and all the kings of ‘Eilam &#91;eternity&#93;, and all the kings of the Medes &#91;middle land&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> And all the kings of the north, far and near, one with another, and all the kingdoms of the world, which &#91;are&#93; upon the face of the earth: and the king of Sheshakh &#91;your fine linen&#93; shall drink last of them &#91;all&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Therefore youi shall say unto them, Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Drink you2f, and be drunken, and spue, and fall, and rise no more, because of the sword which I will send among you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And it shall be, if they refuse to take the cup at youri hand to drink, then shall youi say unto them, Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;; You2f shall certainly drink.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> For, lo, I begin to bring evil on the city which is called by my name, and should you2f be utterly unpunished? </div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Therefore prophesy youi against them all these words, and say unto them, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) shall roar from on high, and utter his voice from his holy habitation; he shall mightily roar upon his habitation; he shall give a shout, as they that trample &#91;the grapes&#93;, against all the inhabitants of the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, Behold, evil shall go forth from nation to nation, and a great whirlwind shall be raised up from the coasts of the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And the slain of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) shall be at that day from &#91;one&#93; end of the earth even unto the &#91;other&#93; end of the earth: they shall not be lamented, neither gathered, nor buried; they shall be dung upon the ground.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> Howl, you2f shepherds, and cry; and wallow (to approve, commend, accept) yourselves &#91;in the ashes&#93;, you2f principal of the flock: for the days of your slaughter and of your dispersions are accomplished; and you2f shall fall like a prized vase.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And the shepherds shall have no way to flee, nor the principal of the flock to escape.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> A voice of the cry of the shepherds, and an howling of the principal of the flock, &#91;shall be heard&#93;: for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has spoiled their pasture.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> And the peaceable habitations are cut down because of the fierce anger of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> He has forsaken his colonnade, as the lion: for their land is desolate because of the fierceness of the oppressor, and because of his fierce anger.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> In the beginning of the reign of Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93; the son of Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; came this word from the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Stand in the court of the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; house, and speak unto all the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, which come to worship in the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; house, all the words that I command youi to speak unto them; diminish not a word:</span>

<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> If so be they will hearken, and turn every man from his evil way, that I may repent me of the evil, which I purpose to do unto them because of the evil of their doings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> To hearken to the words of my servants the prophets, whom I sent unto you, both rising up early, and sending &#91;them&#93;, but you2f have not listened;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> Then will I make this house like Shiloh &#91;Place of Rest&#93;, and will make this city a curse to all the nations of the earth.</span>

<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> For thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; I have put a yoke of iron upon the neck of all these nations, that they may serve N’vukhadnetzar &#91;May Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;; and they shall serve him: and I have given him the beasts of the field also.</span>

</div>

<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> And I will be found of you, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): and I will turn away your captivity, and I will gather you from all the nations, and from all the places to which I have driven you, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); and I will bring you again into the place from what place I caused you to be carried away captive.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, of Ach’av &#91;father’s brother&#93; the son of Kolayah &#91;voice of Yehovah&#93;, and of Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; the son of Ma’aseiyah &#91;work of Yehovah&#93;, which prophesy a lie unto you in my name; Behold, I will deliver them into the hand of N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;; and he shall slay them before your eyes;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> And of them shall be taken up a curse by all the captivity of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; which &#91;are&#93; in Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, saying, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) make youi like Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; and like Ach’av &#91;father’s brother&#93;, whom the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; roasted in the fire;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Because they have committed villainy in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and have committed adultery with their neighbours’ wives, and have spoken lying words in my name, which I have not commanded them; even I know, and &#91;am&#93; a witness, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> &#91;Thus&#93; shall youi also speak to Sh’may’yah &#91;Yehovah hears&#93; the Nechelami &#91;he of the dream&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> Thus speaks the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, saying, Because youi have sent letters in youri name unto all the people that &#91;are&#93; at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and to Tzefan’yahu &#91;Yehovah has treasured&#93; the son of Ma’aseiyah &#91;work of Yehovah&#93; the priest, and to all the priests, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Now therefore why have youi not reproved Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; of ‘Anatot &#91;answers to prayer&#93;, which makes himself a prophet to you?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> For therefore he sent unto us &#91;in&#93; Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, saying, This &#91;captivity is&#93; long: build you2f houses, and dwell &#91;in them&#93;; and plant gardens, and eat the fruit of them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And Tzefan’yahu &#91;Yehovah has treasured&#93; the priest read this letter in the ears of Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; the prophet.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Then came the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> Send to all them of the captivity, saying, Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) concerning Sh’may’yah &#91;Yehovah hears&#93; the Nechelami &#91;he of the dream&#93;; Because that Sh’may’yah &#91;Yehovah hears&#93; has prophesied unto you, and I sent him not, and he caused you to trust in a lie:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Thus speaks the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, saying, Write youi all the words that I have spoken unto youi in a Book.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> For, lo, the days come, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that I will bring again the captivity of my people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): and I will cause them to return to the land that I gave to their fathers, and they shall possess it.</span>

<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> And these &#91;are&#93; the words that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke concerning Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and concerning Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> Alas! for that day &#91;is&#93; great, so that none &#91;is&#93; like it: it &#91;is&#93; even the time of Ya’akov’s &#91;Heal of God&#93;’s trouble; but he shall be saved out of it.</span>

<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> For it shall come to pass in that day, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, &#91;that&#93; I will break his yoke from off youri neck, and will burst youri bonds, and strangers shall no more serve themselves of him:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> But they shall serve the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and David &#91;beloved&#93; their king, whom I will raise up unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> Therefore fear youi not, O my servant Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); neither be dismayed, O Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: for, lo, I will save youi from afar, and youri seed from the land of their captivity; and Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93; shall return, and shall be in rest, and be quiet, and none shall make &#91;him&#93; afraid.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> For I &#91;am&#93; with youi, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), to save youi: though I make a full end of all nations to which I have scattered youi, yet will I not make a full end of youi: but I will correct youi in measure, and will not leave youi altogether unpunished.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> For thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), You1s bruise &#91;is&#93; incurable, &#91;and&#93; youri wound &#91;is&#93; grievous.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> &#91;There is&#93; none to plead youri cause, that youi may be bound up: youi have no healing medicines.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> All youri lovers have forgotten youi; they seek youi not; for I have wounded youi with the wound of an enemy, with the chastisement of a cruel one, for the multitude of youri iniquity; &#91;because&#93; youri sins were increased.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> Why cry youi for youri affliction? youri sorrow &#91;is&#93; incurable for the multitude of youri iniquity: &#91;because&#93; youri sins were increased, I have done these things unto youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> Therefore all they that devour youi shall be devoured; and all youri adversaries, every one of them, shall go into captivity; and they that spoil (plunder; prey; booty) youi shall be a spoil (plunder; prey; booty), and all that prey upon youi will I give for a prey.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> For I will restore health unto youi, and I will heal youi of youri wounds, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); because they called youi an Outcast, &#91;saying&#93;, This &#91;is&#93; Tziyon &#91;parched place&#93;, whom no man seeks after.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Behold, I will bring again the captivity of Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;’s tents, and have mercy on his dwellingplaces; and the city shall be built upon her own heap, and the palace shall remain after the manner thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> And out of them shall proceed thanksgiving and the voice of them that make merry: and I will multiply them, and they shall not be few; I will also glorify them, and they shall not be small.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Their children also shall be as aforetime, and their congregation shall be established before me, and I will punish all that oppress them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> And their nobles shall be of themselves, and their governor shall proceed from the midst of them; and I will cause him to draw near, and he shall approach unto me: for who &#91;is&#93; this that engaged his heart to approach unto me? says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> And you2f shall be my people, and I will be your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Behold, the whirlwind of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) goes forth with fury, a continuing whirlwind: it shall fall with pain upon the head of the wicked.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> The fierce anger of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) shall not return, until he have done &#91;it&#93;, and until he have performed the intents of his heart: in the latter days you2f shall consider it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> At the same time, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), will I be the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of all the families of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and they shall be my people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), The people &#91;which were&#93; left of the sword found grace in the wilderness; &#91;even&#93; Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, when I went to cause him to rest.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has appeared of old unto me, &#91;saying&#93;, Yes, I have loved youi with an everlasting love: therefore with lovingkindness have I drawn youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> Again I will build youi, and youi shall be built, O virgin of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: youi shall again be adorned with youri tambourines, and shall go forth in the dances of them that make merry.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> youi shall yet plant vines upon the mountains of Shomron &#91;watch mountain&#93;: the planters shall plant, and shall eat &#91;them&#93; as common things.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> For there shall be a day, &#91;that&#93; the watchmen upon the mount Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93; shall cry, Arise you2f, and let us go up to Tziyon &#91;parched place&#93; unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> For thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Sing with gladness for Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;, and shout among the chief of the nations: publish you2f, praise you2f, and say, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), save youri people, the remnant of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> Behold, I will bring them from the north country, and gather them from the coasts of the earth, &#91;and&#93; with them the blind and the lame, the woman with child and her that travails with child together: a great company shall return there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> They shall come with weeping, and with supplications will I lead them: I will cause them to walk by the rivers of waters in a straight way, wherein they shall not stumble: for I am a father to Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93; &#91;is&#93; my firstborn.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> Hear the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), O you2f nations, and declare &#91;it&#93; in the isles afar off, and say, He that scattered Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; will gather him, and keep him, as a shepherd &#91;does&#93; his flock.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> For the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has redeemed Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;, and ransomed him from the hand of &#91;him that was&#93; stronger than he.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> Therefore they shall come and sing in the height of Tziyon &#91;parched place&#93;, and shall flow together to the goodness of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), for wheat, and for wine, and for oil, and for the young of the flock and of the herd: and their soul shall be as a watered garden; and they shall not sorrow any more at all.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> Then shall the virgin rejoice in the dance, both young men and old together: for I will turn their mourning into joy, and will comfort them, and make them rejoice from their sorrow.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> And I will satiate the soul of the priests with fatness, and my people shall be satisfied with my goodness, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); A voice was heard in Ramah &#91;Hill&#93;, lamentation, &#91;and&#93; bitter weeping; Rachel &#91;ewe&#93; weeping for her children refused to be comforted for her children, because they &#91;were&#93; no longer alive.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Refrain youri voice from weeping, and youri eyes from tears: for youri work shall be rewarded, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); and they shall come again from the land of the enemy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> And there is hope in youri end, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that youri children shall come again to their own border.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> I have surely heard Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93; bemoaning himself &#91;thus&#93;; youi have chastised me, and I was chastised, as a bullock unaccustomed &#91;to the yoke&#93;: turn youi me, and I shall be turned; for youi &#91;are&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> Surely after that I was turned, I repented; and after that I was instructed, I struck upon &#91;my&#93; thigh: I was ashamed, yes, even confounded, because I did bear the reproach (disgrace; shame) of my youth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> &#91;Is&#93; Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93; my dear son? &#91;is he&#93; a pleasant child? for since I spoke against him, I do earnestly remember him still: therefore my bowels (inward parts; affections) are troubled for him; I will surely have mercy upon him, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Set youi up waymarks, make youri high heaps: set youri heart toward the highway, &#91;even&#93; the way &#91;which&#93; youi went: turn again, O virgin of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, turn again to these youri cities.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> How long will youi go about, O youi backsliding daughter? for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has created a new thing in the earth, A woman shall compass (go around, encircle; encompass) a man.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; As yet they shall use this speech in the land of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and in the cities thereof, when I shall bring again their captivity; The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) bless youi, O habitation of justice, &#91;and&#93; mountain of holiness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And there shall dwell in Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; itself, and in all the cities thereof together, husbandmen, and they &#91;that&#93; go forth with flocks.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> For I have satiated the weary soul, and I have replenished every sorrowful soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Upon this I awakened, and beheld; and my sleep was sweet unto me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Behold, the days come, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that I will sow the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and the house of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; with the seed of man, and with the seed of beast.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And it shall come to pass, &#91;that&#93; like as I have watched over them, to pluck up, and to break down, and to throw down, and to destroy, and to afflict; so will I watch over them, to build, and to plant, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> In those days they shall say no more, The fathers have eaten a sour grape, and the children’s teeth are set on edge.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> But everyone shall die for his own iniquity: every man that eats the sour grape, his teeth shall be set on edge.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> Behold, the days come, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that I will make a new covenant with the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and with the house of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Not according to the covenant that I made with their fathers in the day &#91;that&#93; I took them by the hand to bring them out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;; which my covenant they broke, although I was an husband unto them, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate):</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> But this &#91;shall be&#93; the covenant that I will make with the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; After those days, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), I will put my law in their inward parts, and write it in their hearts; and will be their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and they shall be my people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And they shall teach no more every man his neighbour, and every man his brother, saying, Know the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): for they shall all know me, from the least of them unto the greatest of them, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): for I will forgive their iniquity, and I will remember their sin no more.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), which gives the sun for a light by day, &#91;and&#93; the ordinances of the moon and of the stars for a light by night, which divides the sea when the waves thereof roar; The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93; &#91;is&#93; his name:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> If those ordinances depart from before me, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), &#91;then&#93; the seed of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; also shall cease from being a nation before me forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); If heaven above can be measured, and the foundations of the earth searched out beneath, I will also cast off all the seed of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; for all that they have done, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> Behold, the days come, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that the city shall be built to the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) from the Tower of Hanan’el &#91;God is favoured&#93; unto the gate of the corner.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> And the measuring line shall yet go forth over against it upon the hill Garev &#91;scabby&#93;, and shall compass (go around, encircle; encompass) about to Go’ah &#91;bellowing&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> And the whole valley of the dead bodies, and of the ashes, and all the fields unto the brook of Kidron &#91;dark&#93;, unto the corner of the horse gate toward the east, &#91;shall be&#93; holy unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); it shall not be plucked up, nor thrown down any more forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> The word that came to Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; from the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in the tenth year of Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, which &#91;was&#93; the eighteenth year of N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93;.</span>
</div>
