<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> For then the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;’s army besieged Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;: and Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; the prophet was shut up in the court of the prison, which &#91;was&#93; in the king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;’s house.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> For Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; had shut him up, saying, For what reason do youi prophesy, and say, Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), Behold, I will give this city into the hand of the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and he shall take it;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> And Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; shall not escape out of the hand of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;, but shall surely be delivered into the hand of the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and shall speak with him mouth to mouth, and his eyes shall behold his eyes;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> And he shall lead Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; to Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and there shall he be until I visit him, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): though you2f fight with the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;, you2f shall not prosper.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> And Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; said, The word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> Behold, ‘Hanam’el &#91;God is gracious&#93; the son of Shalum &#91;retribution&#93; youri uncle shall come unto youi, saying, Buy youi my field that &#91;is&#93; in ‘Anatot &#91;answers to prayer&#93;: for the right of redemption &#91;is&#93; yoursi, so buy &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> So ‘Hanam’el &#91;God is gracious&#93; my uncle’s son came to me in the court of the prison according to the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and said unto me, Buy my field, I pray youi, that &#91;is&#93; in ‘Anatot &#91;answers to prayer&#93;, which &#91;is&#93; in the country of Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93;: for the right of inheritance &#91;is&#93; yoursi, and the redemption &#91;is&#93; yoursi; buy &#91;it&#93; for yourself. Then I knew that this &#91;was&#93; the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> And I bought the field of ‘Hanam’el &#91;God is gracious&#93; my uncle’s son, that &#91;was&#93; in ‘Anatot &#91;answers to prayer&#93;, and weighed him the money, &#91;even&#93; seventeen shekels of silver.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> And I subscribed the evidence, and sealed &#91;it&#93;, and took witnesses, and weighed &#91;him&#93; the money in the balances.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> So I took the evidence of the purchase, &#91;both&#93; that which was sealed &#91;according&#93; to the law and custom, and that which was open:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> And I gave the evidence of the purchase unto Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93; the son of Neriyah &#91;lamp of Yehovah&#93;, the son of Ma’aseiyah &#91;work of Yehovah&#93;, in the sight of ‘Hanam’el &#91;God is gracious&#93; my uncle’s &#91;son&#93;, and in the presence of the witnesses that subscribed the book of the purchase, before all the Y’hudim (Judeans) that sat in the court of the prison.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> And I charged Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93; before them, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Take these evidences, this evidence of the purchase, both which is sealed, and this evidence which is open; and put them in an earthen vessel, that they may continue many days.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> For thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Houses and fields and vineyards shall be possessed again in this land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> Now when I had delivered the evidence of the purchase unto Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93; the son of Neriyah &#91;lamp of Yehovah&#93;, I prayed unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> Ah Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER)! behold, youi have made the heaven and the earth by youri great power and stretched out arm, &#91;and&#93; there is nothing too hard for youi:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> youi show lovingkindness unto thousands, and recompense the iniquity of the fathers into the bosom of their children after them: the Great, the Mighty God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, &#91;is&#93; His Name,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> Great in counsel, and mighty in work: for youri eyes &#91;are&#93; open upon all the ways of the sons of men: to give everyone according to his ways, and according to the fruit of his doings:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Which have set signs and wonders in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, &#91;even&#93; unto this day, and in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and among &#91;other&#93; men; and have made youi a name, as at this day;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> And have brought forth youri people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; with signs, and with wonders, and with a strong hand, and with a stretched out arm, and with great terror;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> And have given them this land, which youi did swear to their fathers to give them, a land flowing with milk and honey;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And they came in, and possessed it; but they obeyed not youri voice, neither walked in youi law; they have done nothing of all that youi commanded them to do: therefore youi have caused all this evil to come upon them:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Behold the mounts, they are come unto the city to take it; and the city is given into the hand of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;, that fight against it, because of the sword, and of the famine, and of the pestilence: and what youi have spoken is come to pass; and, behold, youi see &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And youi have said unto me, O Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), Buy youi the field for money, and take witnesses; for the city is given into the hand of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Then came the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Behold, I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of all flesh: is there anything too hard for me?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Behold, I will give this city into the hand of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;, and into the hand of N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and he shall take it:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;, that fight against this city, shall come and set fire on this city, and burn it with the houses, upon whose roofs they have offered incense unto Ba’al &#91;lord (a false god)&#93;, and poured out drink offerings unto other gods, to provoke me to anger.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> For the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and the children of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; have only done evil before me from their youth: for the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; have only provoked me to anger with the work of their hands, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> For this city has been to me &#91;as&#93; a provocation of my anger and of my fury from the day that they built it even unto this day; that I should remove it from before my face,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Because of all the evil of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and of the children of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, which they have done to provoke me to anger, they, their kings, their princes, their priests, and their prophets, and the men of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And they have turned unto me the back, and not the face: though I taught them, rising up early and teaching &#91;them&#93;, yet they have not listened to receive instruction.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> But they set their abominations in the house, which is called by my name, to defile it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And they built the high places of Ba’al &#91;lord (a false god)&#93;, which &#91;are&#93; in the valley of the son of Hinnom &#91;Lamentation&#93;, to cause their sons and their daughters to pass through &#91;the fire&#93; unto Molekh &#91;king&#93;; which I commanded them not, neither came it into my mind, that they should do this abomination, to cause Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; to sin.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And now therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, concerning this city, whereof you2f say, It shall be delivered into the hand of the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; by the sword, and by the famine, and by the pestilence;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> Behold, I will gather them out of all countries, to which I have driven them in my anger, and in my fury, and in great wrath; and I will bring them again unto this place, and I will cause them to dwell safely:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> And they shall be my people, and I will be their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> And I will give them one heart, and one way, that they may fear me forever, for the good of them, and of their children after them:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> And I will make an everlasting covenant with them, that I will not turn away from them, to do them good; but I will put my fear in their hearts, that they shall not depart from me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> Yes, I will rejoice over them to do them good, and I will plant them in this land assuredly with my whole heart and with my whole soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> For thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Like as I have brought all this great evil upon this people, so will I bring upon them all the good that I have promised them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> And fields shall be bought in this land, whereof you2f say, &#91;It is&#93; desolate without man or beast; it is given into the hand of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> Men shall buy fields for money, and subscribe evidences, and seal &#91;them&#93;, and take witnesses in the land of Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93;, and in the places about Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and in the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and in the cities of the mountains, and in the cities of the valley, and in the cities of the south: for I will cause their captivity to return, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> Moreover the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; the second time, while he was yet shut up in the court of the prison, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) the maker thereof, the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) that formed it, to establish it; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;is&#93; his name;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> Call unto me, and I will answer youi, and show youi great and mighty things, which youi know not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> For thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, concerning the houses of this city, and concerning the houses of the kings of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, which are thrown down by the mounts, and by the sword;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> They come to fight with the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;, but &#91;it is&#93; to fill them with the dead bodies of men, whom I have slain in my anger and in my fury, and for all whose wickedness I have hid my face from this city.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> Behold, I will bring it health and cure, and I will cure them, and will reveal unto them the abundance of peace and truth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> And I will cause the captivity of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and the captivity of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; to return, and will build them, as at the first.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> And I will cleanse them from all their iniquity, whereby they have sinned against me; and I will pardon all their iniquities, whereby they have sinned, and whereby they have transgressed against me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> And it shall be to me a name of joy, a praise and an honour before all the nations of the earth, which shall hear all the good that I do unto them: and they shall fear and tremble for all the goodness and for all the prosperity that I procure unto it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Again there shall be heard in this place, which you2f say &#91;shall be&#93; desolate without man and without beast, &#91;even&#93; in the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and in the streets of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, that are desolate, without man, and without inhabitant, and without beast,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> The voice of joy, and the voice of gladness, the voice of the bridegroom, and the voice of the bride, the voice of them that shall say, Praise the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;: for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;is&#93; good; for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever: &#91;and&#93; of them that shall bring the sacrifice of praise into the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate). For I will cause to return the captivity of the land, as at the first, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;; Again in this place, which is desolate without man and without beast, and in all the cities thereof, shall be an habitation of shepherds causing &#91;their&#93; flocks to lie down.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> In the cities of the mountains, in the cities of the vale, and in the cities of the south, and in the land of Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93;, and in the places about Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and in the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, shall the flocks pass again under the hands of him that tells &#91;them&#93;, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> Behold, the days come, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that I will perform that good thing which I have promised unto the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and to the house of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> In those days, and at that time, will I cause the Branch of Righteousness to grow up unto David &#91;beloved&#93;; and he shall execute judgment and righteousness in the land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> In those days shall Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; be saved, and Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; shall dwell safely: and this &#91;is the name&#93; which she shall be called, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our righteousness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> For thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); David &#91;beloved&#93; shall never lack a man to sit upon the throne of the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> Neither shall the priests the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; lack a man before me to offer burnt offerings, and to kindle meat offerings, and to do sacrifice continually.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> And the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); If you2f can break my covenant of the day, and my covenant of the night, and that there should not be day and night in their season;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> &#91;Then&#93; may also my covenant be broken with David &#91;beloved&#93; my servant, that he should not have a son to reign upon his throne; and with the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; the priests, my ministers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> As the army of heaven cannot be numbered, neither the sand of the sea measured: so will I multiply the seed of David &#91;beloved&#93; my servant, and the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; that minister unto me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Moreover the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came to Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Consider youi not what this people have spoken, saying, The two families which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has chosen, he has even cast them off? thus they have despised my people, that they should be no more a nation before them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); If my covenant &#91;be&#93; not with day and night, &#91;and if&#93; I have not appointed the ordinances of heaven and earth;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Then will I cast away the seed of Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;, and David &#91;beloved&#93; my servant, &#91;so&#93; that I will not take &#91;any&#93; of his seed &#91;to be&#93; rulers over the seed of Avraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93;, Yitz’khak &#91;Laughter&#93;, and Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;: for I will cause their captivity to return, and have mercy on them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> The word which came unto Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; from the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), when N’vukhadnetzar &#91;May Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and all his army, and all the kingdoms of the earth of his dominion, and all the people, fought against Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and against all the cities thereof, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Go and speak to Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and tell him, Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Behold, I will give this city into the hand of the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and he shall burn it with fire:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> And youi shall not escape out of his hand, but shall surely be taken, and delivered into his hand; and youri eyes shall behold the eyes of the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and he shall speak with youi mouth to mouth, and youi shall go to Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> Yet hear the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), O Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;; Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) of youi, youi shall not die by the sword:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> &#91;But&#93; youi shall die in peace: and with the burnings of youri fathers, the former kings which were before youi, so shall they burn &#91;odours&#93; for youi; and they will lament youi, &#91;saying&#93;, Ah lord! for I have pronounced the word, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> Then Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; the prophet spoke all these words unto Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> When the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;’s army fought against Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and against all the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; that were left, against Lakhish &#91;Invincible&#93;, and against ‘Azekah &#91;Dug Over&#93;: for these fortified cities remained of the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> &#91;This is&#93; the word that came unto Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; from the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), after that the king Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; had made a covenant with all the people which &#91;were&#93; at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, to proclaim liberty unto them;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> That every man should let his manservant, and every man his maidservant, &#91;being&#93; an ‘Evri’ot &#91;Hebrew male&#93; or an ‘Evri’ah &#91;Hebrew female&#93;, go free; that none should serve himself of them, &#91;that is to say&#93;, of a Y’hudi (Jewish person) his brother.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> Now when all the princes, and all the people, which had entered into the covenant, heard that everyone should let his manservant, and everyone his maidservant, go free, that none should serve themselves of them anymore, then they obeyed, and let &#91;them&#93; go.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> But afterward they turned, and caused the servants and the handmaids, whom they had let go free, to return, and brought them into subjection for servants and for handmaids.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> Therefore the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came to Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; from the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; I made a covenant with your fathers in the day that I brought them forth out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, out of the house of bondmen, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> At the end of seven years let you2f go every man his brother an ‘Evri’ot &#91;Hebrew&#93;, which has been sold unto youi; and when he has served youi six years, youi shall let him go free from youi: but your fathers listened not unto me, neither inclined their ear.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> And you2f were now turned, and had done right in my sight, in proclaiming liberty every man to his neighbour; and you2f had made a covenant before me in the house which is called by my name:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> But you2f turned and polluted my name, and caused every man his servant, and every man his female servant, whom you2f had set at liberty at their pleasure, to return, and brought them into subjection, to be unto you for servants and for handmaids.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); You2f have not listened unto me, in proclaiming liberty, everyone to his brother, and every man to his neighbour: behold, I proclaim a liberty for you, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), to the sword, to the pestilence, and to the famine; and I will make you to be removed into all the kingdoms of the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> And I will give the men that have transgressed my covenant, which have not performed the words of the covenant which they had made before me, when they cut the calf in two, and passed between the parts thereof,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> The princes of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and the princes of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, the eunuchs, and the priests, and all the people of the land, which passed between the parts of the calf;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> I will even give them into the hand of their enemies, and into the hand of them that seek their life: and their dead bodies shall be for meat unto the fowls of the heaven, and to the beasts of the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> And Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and his princes will I give into the hand of their enemies, and into the hand of them that seek their life, and into the hand of the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;’s army, which are gone up from you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Behold, I will command, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and cause them to return to this city; and they shall fight against it, and take it, and burn it with fire: and I will make the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; a desolation without an inhabitant.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> The word which came unto Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; from the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in the days of Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93; the son of Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Go unto the house of the Rekhavim &#91;uttermost part&#93;, and speak unto them, and bring them into the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), into one of the chambers, and give them wine to drink.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> Then I took Ya’azanyahu &#91;Yehovah hears&#93; the son of Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;, the son of Havatzinyah &#91;light of Yehovah&#93;, and his brethren, and all his sons, and the whole house of the Rekhavim &#91;uttermost part&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> And I brought them into the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), into the chamber of the sons of Hanan &#91;he is merciful&#93;, the son of Yigdalyahu &#91;Yehovah is great&#93;, a man of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, which &#91;was&#93; by the chamber of the princes, which &#91;was&#93; above the chamber of Ma’aseiyah &#91;work of Yehovah&#93; the son of Shalum &#91;retribution&#93;, the keeper of the door:</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> And I set before the sons of the house of the Rekhavim &#91;uttermost part&#93; pots full of wine, and cups, and I said unto them, Drink you2f wine.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> But they said, We will drink no wine: for Yonadav &#91;Yehovah is willing&#93; &#91;Generous&#93; the son of Rekhav &#91;rider&#93; our father commanded us, saying, You2f shall drink no wine, &#91;neither you2f&#93;, nor your sons forever:</span>

<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> Neither shall you2f build house, nor sow seed, nor plant vineyard, nor have &#91;any&#93;: but all your days you2f shall dwell in tents; that you2f may live many days in the land where you2f &#91;be&#93; strangers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> Thus have we obeyed the voice of Yonadav &#91;Yehovah is willing&#93; &#91;Generous&#93; the son of Rekhav &#91;rider&#93; our father in all that he has charged us, to drink no wine all our days, we, our wives, our sons, nor our daughters;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> Nor to build houses for us to dwell in: neither have we vineyard, nor field, nor seed:</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> But it came to pass, when N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; came up into the land, that we said, Come, and let us go to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; for fear of the army of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;, and for fear of the army of the Aram &#91;Syrians&#93;: so we dwell at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> Then came the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Go and tell the men of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, Will you2f not receive instruction to hearken to my words? says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> The words of Yonadav &#91;Yehovah is willing&#93; &#91;Generous&#93; the son of Rekhav &#91;rider&#93;, that he commanded his sons not to drink wine, are performed; for unto this day they drink none, but obey their father’s commandment: notwithstanding I have spoken unto you, rising early and speaking; but you2f listened not unto me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> I have sent also unto you all my servants the prophets, rising up early and sending &#91;them&#93;, saying, Return you2f now every man from his evil way, and amend your doings, and go not after other gods to serve them, and you2f shall dwell in the land which I have given to you and to your fathers: but you2f have not inclined your ear, nor listened unto me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> Because the sons of Yonadav &#91;Yehovah is willing&#93; &#91;Generous&#93; the son of Rekhav &#91;rider&#93; have performed the commandment of their father, which he commanded them; but this people has not listened unto me:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Behold, I will bring upon Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and upon all the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; all the evil that I have pronounced against them: because I have spoken unto them, but they have not heard; and I have called unto them, but they have not answered.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> And Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; said unto the house of the Rekhavim &#91;uttermost part&#93;, Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Because you2f have obeyed the commandment of Yonadav &#91;Yehovah is willing&#93; &#91;Generous&#93; your father, and kept all his precepts, and done according unto all that he has commanded you:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Yonadav &#91;Yehovah is willing&#93; &#91;Generous&#93; the son of Rekhav &#91;rider&#93; shall not lack a man to stand before me forever.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Take youi a roll of a book, and write in it all the words that I have spoken unto youi against Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and against Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and against all the nations, from the day I spoke unto youi, from the days of Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93;, even unto this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> It may be that the house of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; will hear all the evil which I purpose to do unto them; that they may return every man from his evil way; that I may forgive their iniquity and their sin.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> Then Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; called Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93; the son of Neriyah &#91;lamp of Yehovah&#93;: and Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93; wrote from the mouth of Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; all the words of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), which he had spoken unto him, upon a roll of a book.</span>

<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> And Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; commanded Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93;, saying, I &#91;am&#93; shut up; I cannot go into the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate):</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> Therefore go youi, and read in the roll, which youi have written from my mouth, the words of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in the ears of the people in the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; house upon the fasting day: and also youi shall read them in the ears of all Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; that come out of their cities.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> And Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93; the son of Neriyah &#91;lamp of Yehovah&#93; did according to all that Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; the prophet commanded him, reading in the Book the words of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; house.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> And it came to pass in the fifth year of Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93; the son of Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, in the ninth month, &#91;that&#93; they proclaimed a fast before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) to all the people in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and to all the people that came from the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; unto Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> Then read Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93; in the book the words of Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; in the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), in the chamber of G’maryah &#91;Yehovah has accomplished&#93; the son of Shafan &#91;coney&#93; the scribe, in the higher court, at the entry of the new gate of the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; house, in the ears of all the people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> When Mikhayah &#91;who is like God?&#93; the son of G’maryah &#91;Yehovah has accomplished&#93;, the son of Shafan &#91;coney&#93;, had heard out of the book all the words of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate),</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> Then he went down into the king’s house, into the scribe’s chamber: and, lo, all the princes sat there, &#91;even&#93; Elishama &#91;My God has Heard&#93; the scribe, and D’layahu &#91;Yehovah has accomplished&#93; the son of Sh’may’yah &#91;Yehovah hears&#93;, and Elnatan &#91;God has given&#93; the son of ‘Akhbor &#91;mouse&#93;, and G’maryah &#91;Yehovah has accomplished&#93; the son of Shafan &#91;coney&#93;, and Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; the son of Hananyah &#91;God has favoured&#93;, and all the princes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> Then Mikhayah &#91;who is like God?&#93; declared unto them all the words that he had heard, when Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93; read the book in the ears of the people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> Therefore all the princes sent Y’hudi &#91;the Jewish one&#93; the son of N’tanyah &#91;given of Yehovah&#93;, the son of Shelemyah &#91;repaid by Yehovah&#93; &#91;repaid by Yehovah&#93;, the son of Kushi &#91;their blackness&#93;, unto Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93;, saying, Take in youri hand the roll wherein youi have read in the ears of the people, and come. So Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93; the son of Neriyah &#91;lamp of Yehovah&#93; took the roll in his hand, and came unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> And they said unto him, Sit down now, and read it in our ears. So Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93; read &#91;it&#93; in their ears.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> Now it came to pass, when they had heard all the words, they were afraid both one and other, and said unto Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93;, We will surely tell the king of all these words.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> And they asked Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93;, saying, Tell us now, How did youi write all these words at his mouth?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> Then Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93; answered them, He pronounced all these words unto me with his mouth, and I wrote &#91;them&#93; with ink in the book.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> Then said the princes unto Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93;, Go, hide youi, youi and Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;; and let no man know where you2f be.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> And they went in to the king into the court, but they laid up the roll in the chamber of Elishama &#91;My God has Heard&#93; the scribe, and told all the words in the ears of the king.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> So the king sent Y’hudi &#91;the Jewish one&#93; to fetch the roll: and he took it out of Elishama &#91;My God has Heard&#93; the scribe’s chamber. And Y’hudi &#91;the Jewish one&#93; read it in the ears of the king, and in the ears of all the princes which stood beside the king.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Now the king sat in the winter house in the ninth month: and &#91;there was a fire&#93; on the hearth burning before him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And it came to pass, &#91;that&#93; when Y’hudi &#91;the Jewish one&#93; had read three or four leaves, he cut it with the penknife, and cast &#91;it&#93; into the fire that &#91;was&#93; on the hearth, until all the roll was consumed in the fire that &#91;was&#93; on the hearth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Yet they were not afraid, nor rent their garments, &#91;neither&#93; the king, nor any of his servants that heard all these words.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> Nevertheless Elnatan &#91;God has given&#93; and D’layahu &#91;Yehovah has accomplished&#93; and G’maryah &#91;Yehovah has accomplished&#93; had made intercession to the king that he would not burn the roll: but he would not hear them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> But the king commanded Yerachme’el &#91;my God have pity&#93; the son of the king, and S’rayah &#91;Yehovah is ruler&#93; the son of ‘Azri’el &#91;my help is God&#93;, and Shelemyah &#91;repaid by Yehovah&#93; &#91;repaid by Yehovah&#93; the son of ‘Acde’el &#91;servant of God&#93;, to take Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93; the scribe and Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; the prophet: but the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) hid them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Then the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came to Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;, after that the king had burned the roll, and the words which Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93; wrote at the mouth of Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> Take youi again another roll, and write in it all the former words that were in the first roll, which Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93; the king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; has burned.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And youi shall say to Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); youi have burned this roll, saying, Why have youi written in it, saying, The king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; shall certainly come and destroy this land, and shall cause to cease from there man and beast?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) of Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;; He shall have none to sit upon the throne of David &#91;beloved&#93;: and his dead body shall be cast out in the day to the heat, and in the night to the frost.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And I will punish him and his seed and his servants for their iniquity; and I will bring upon them, and upon the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and upon the men of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, all the evil that I have pronounced against them; but they listened not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Then took Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; another roll, and gave it to Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93; the scribe, the son of Neriyah &#91;lamp of Yehovah&#93;; who wrote in it from the mouth of Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; all the words of the book which Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; had burned in the fire: and there were added besides unto them many like words.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> And king Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; the son of Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; reigned instead of Koniyahu &#91;Yehovah will establish&#93; the son of Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93;, whom N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; made king in the land of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> But neither he, nor his servants, nor the people of the land, did hearken unto the words of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), which he spoke by the prophet Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> Then Par’oh’s &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; army was come forth out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;: and when the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93; that besieged Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; heard tidings of them, they departed from Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>

</div>

<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Deceive not yourselves, saying, The Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93; shall surely depart from us: for they shall not depart.</span>
</div>

But he listened not to him: so Yir’iyah &#91;speak&#93; took Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;, and brought him to the princes.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>

And Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; said, There is: for, said he, youi shall be delivered into the hand of the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> Moreover Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; said unto king Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93;, What have I offended against youi, or against youri servants, or against this people, that you2f have put me in prison?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> Where &#91;are&#93; now your prophets which prophesied unto you, saying, The king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; shall not come against you, nor against this land?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Therefore hear now, I pray youi, O my lord the king: let my supplication, I pray youi, be accepted before youi; that youi cause me not to return to the house of Y’honatan &#91;Yehovah has Given&#93; the scribe, lest I die there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

Thus Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; remained in the court of the prison.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> Then Sh’fatyah &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; the son of Mattan &#91;a gift&#93;, and G’dalyahu &#91;Yehovah is great&#93; the son of Pash’chur &#91;freedom&#93;, and Yukhal &#91;Yehovah is able&#93; the son of Shelemyah &#91;repaid by Yehovah&#93; &#91;repaid by Yehovah&#93;, and Pash’chur &#91;freedom&#93; the son of Malkiyah &#91;my king is Yehovah&#93;, heard the words that Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; had spoken unto all the people, saying,</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), He that remains in this city shall die by the sword, by the famine, and by the pestilence: but he that goes forth to the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93; shall live; for he shall have his life for a prey, and shall live.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), This city shall surely be given into the hand of the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;’s army, which shall take it.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> So they drew up Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; with cords, and took him up out of the dungeon: and Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; remained in the court of the prison.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> Then Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; the king sent, and took Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; the prophet unto him into the third entry that &#91;is&#93; in the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): and the king said unto Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;, I will ask youi a thing; hide nothing from me.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> But if youi refuse to go forth, this &#91;is&#93; the word that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has showed me:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> And, behold, all the women that are left in the king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;’s house &#91;shall be&#93; brought forth to the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;’s princes, and those &#91;women&#93; shall say, Your1s friends have set youi on, and have prevailed against youi: youri feet are sunk in the mire, &#91;and&#93; they are turned away back.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> So they shall bring out all youri wives and youri children to the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;: and youi shall not escape out of their hand, but shall be taken by the hand of the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;: and youi shall cause this city to be burned with fire.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Then said Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; unto Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;, Let no man know of these words, and youi shall not die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Then came all the princes unto Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;, and asked him: and he told them according to all these words that the king had commanded. So they left off speaking with him; for the matter was not perceived.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> In the ninth year of Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, in the tenth month, came N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; and all his army against Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and they besieged it.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> And all the princes of the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; came in, and sat in the middle gate, &#91;even&#93; Nergal-Sar’etzer &#91;prince of fire&#93;, Samgar-N’vo &#91;sword of Nebo&#93;, Sars’khim &#91;prince of eunuchs&#93;, Rav-Saris &#91;chief eunuch&#93;, Nergal-Sar’etzer &#91;prince of fire&#93;, Rav-Mag &#91;chief magician&#93;, with all the residue (part that remains, the rest) of the princes of the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> And it came to pass, &#91;that&#93; when Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; the king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; saw them, and all the men of war, then they fled, and went forth out of the city by night, by the way of the king’s garden, by the gate between the two walls: and he went out the way of the plain.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> But the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93; army pursued after them, and overtook Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; in the plains of Yericho &#91;Its Moon&#93;: and when they had taken him, they brought him up to N’vukhadnetzar &#91;May Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; to Rivlah &#91;Fertility&#93; in the land of Hamat &#91;Fortress&#93;, where he gave judgment upon him.</span>

<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> For thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; As my anger and my fury has been poured forth upon the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;; so shall my fury be poured forth upon you, when you2f shall enter into Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;: and you2f shall be an execration, and an astonishment, and a curse, and a reproach (disgrace; shame); and you2f shall see this place no more.</span>

<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> The word that came to Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; concerning all the Y’hudim (Judeans) which dwell in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, which dwell at Migdol &#91;Tower&#93;, and at Tachpanches &#91;you will fill hands with pity&#93;, and at Nof &#91;presentability&#93;, and in the country of Patros &#91;region of the south&#93;, saying,</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; You2f have seen all the evil that I have brought upon Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and upon all the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;; and, behold, this day they &#91;are&#93; a desolation, and no man dwells in it,</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> Because of their wickedness which they have committed to provoke me to anger, in that they went to burn incense, &#91;and&#93; to serve other gods, whom they knew not, &#91;neither&#93; they, you2f, nor your fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> For what reason my fury and my anger was poured forth, and was kindled in the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and in the streets of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;; and they are wasted &#91;and&#93; desolate, as at this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> Therefore now thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; For what reason commit you2f &#91;this&#93; great evil against your souls, to cut off from you man and woman, child and suckling, out of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, to leave you none to remain;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> In that you2f provoke me unto wrath with the works of your hands, burning incense unto other gods in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, to which you2f be gone to dwell, that you2f might cut yourselves off, and that you2f might be a curse and a reproach (disgrace; shame) among all the nations of the earth?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> Have you2f forgotten the wickedness of your fathers, and the wickedness of the kings of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and the wickedness of their wives, and your own wickedness, and the wickedness of your wives, which they have committed in the land of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and in the streets of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> They are not humbled &#91;even&#93; unto this day, neither have they feared, nor walked in my law, nor in my statutes, that I set before you and before your fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Behold, I will set my face against you for evil, and to cut off all Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> And I will take the remnant of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, that have set their faces to go into the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; to sojourn there, and they shall all be consumed, &#91;and&#93; fall in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;; they shall &#91;even&#93; be consumed by the sword &#91;and&#93; by the famine: they shall die, from the least even unto the greatest, by the sword and by the famine: and they shall be an execration, &#91;and&#93; an astonishment, and a curse, and a reproach (disgrace; shame).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> For I will punish them that dwell in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, as I have punished Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, by the sword, by the famine, and by the pestilence:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> So that none of the remnant of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, which are gone into the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; to sojourn there, shall escape or remain, that they should return into the land of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, to the which they have a desire to return to dwell there: for none shall return but such as shall escape.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> &#91;As for&#93; the word that youi have spoken unto us in the name of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), we will not hearken unto youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> But we will certainly do whatsoever thing goes forth out of our own mouth, to burn incense unto the queen of heaven, and to pour out drink offerings unto her, as we have done, we, and our fathers, our kings, and our princes, in the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and in the streets of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;: for &#91;then&#93; had we plenty of victuals (food for humans), and were well, and saw no evil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> But since we left off to burn incense to the queen of heaven, and to pour out drink offerings unto her, we have wanted all &#91;things&#93;, and have been consumed by the sword and by the famine.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> And when we burned incense to the queen of heaven, and poured out drink offerings unto her, did we make her cakes to worship her, and pour out drink offerings unto her, without our men?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Then Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; said unto all the people, to the men, and to the women, and to all the people which had given him &#91;that&#93; answer, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> The incense that you2f burned in the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and in the streets of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, you2f, and your fathers, your kings, and your princes, and the people of the land, did not the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) remember them, and came it &#91;not&#93; into his mind?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> So that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) could no longer bear, because of the evil of your doings, &#91;and&#93; because of the abominations which you2f have committed; therefore is your land a desolation, and an astonishment, and a curse, without an inhabitant, as at this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Because you2f have burned incense, and because you2f have sinned against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and have not obeyed the voice of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), nor walked in his law, nor in his statutes, nor in his testimonies; therefore this evil is happened unto you, as at this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Moreover Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; said unto all the people, and to all the women, Hear the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), all Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; that &#91;are&#93; in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, saying; You2f and your wives have both spoken with your mouths, and fulfilled with your hand, saying, We will surely perform our vows that we have vowed, to burn incense to the queen of heaven, and to pour out drink offerings unto her: you2f will surely accomplish your vows, and surely perform your vows.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Therefore hear you2f the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), all Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; that dwell in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;; Behold, I have sworn by my great name, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that my name shall no more be named in the mouth of any man of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; in all the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, saying, The Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER) lives.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Behold, I will watch over them for evil, and not for good: and all the men of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; that &#91;are&#93; in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; shall be consumed by the sword and by the famine, until there be an end of them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> Yet a small number that escape the sword shall return out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; into the land of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and all the remnant of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, that are gone into the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; to sojourn there, shall know whose words shall stand, my, or theirs.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And this &#91;shall be&#93; a sign unto you, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that I will punish you in this place, that you2f may know that my words shall surely stand against you for evil:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Behold, I will give Pharaoh Hofra &#91;the great house - covering evil&#93; king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; into the hand of his enemies, and into the hand of them that seek his life; as I gave Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; into the hand of N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, his enemy, and that sought his life.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> The word that Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; the prophet spoke unto Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93; the son of Neriyah &#91;lamp of Yehovah&#93;, when he had written these words in a book at the mouth of Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93;, in the fourth year of Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93; the son of Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, unto youi, O Barukh &#91;Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> youi did say, Woe is me now! for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has added grief to my sorrow; I fainted in my sighing, and I find no rest.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> Thus shall youi say unto him, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) says thus; Behold, &#91;that&#93; which I have built will I break down, and that which I have planted I will pluck up, even this whole land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> And seek youi great things for yourself? seek &#91;them&#93; not: for, behold, I will bring evil upon all flesh, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): but youi life will I give unto youi for a prey in all places to which youi go.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> The word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) which came to Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; the prophet against the Gentiles;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Against Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, against the army of Pharaoh N’kho &#91;great house - he is smitten&#93; king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, which was by the river Euphrates (to break forth, to gush) in Kark’mish &#91;fortress of Chemosh (subdue)&#93;, which N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; struck in the fourth year of Y’hoyakim &#91;Yehovah raises up&#93; the son of Yoshiyahu &#91;whom Yehovah heals&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> Order you2f the buckler (round shielf held with a grip) and shield, and draw near to battle.</span>
</div>
