</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> For what reason have I seen them dismayed &#91;and&#93; turned away back? and their mighty ones are beaten down, and are fled apace (at a quick pace; quickly), and look not back: &#91;for&#93; fear &#91;was&#93; round about, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> Let not the swift flee away, nor the mighty man escape; they shall stumble, and fall to (to do entirely, do wholly) ward (in custody, with a guard; prison) the north by the river Euphrates (to break forth, to gush).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> Who &#91;is&#93; this &#91;that&#93; comes up as a flood, whose waters are moved as the rivers?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; rises up like a flood, and &#91;his&#93; waters are moved like the rivers; and he says, I will go up, &#91;and&#93; will cover the earth; I will destroy the city and the inhabitants thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> Come up, you2f horses; and rage, you2f chariots; and let the mighty men come forth; the Ethiopians and the Libyans, that handle the shield; and the Ludim &#91;strife&#93;, that handle &#91;and&#93; bend the bow.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> For this &#91;is&#93; the day of the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, a day of vengeance, that he may avenge him of his adversaries: and the sword shall devour, and it shall be satiate and made drunk with their blood: for the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93; has a sacrifice in the north country by the river Euphrates (to break forth, to gush).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> Go up into Gil’ad &#91;Rocky Region&#93;, and take balm (a medicine; rosin from a bush), O virgin, the daughter of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;: in vain shall youi use many medicines; &#91;for&#93; youi shall not be cured.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> The nations have heard of youri shame, and youri cry has filled the land: for the mighty man has stumbled against the mighty, &#91;and&#93; they are fallen both together.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> The word that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke to Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; the prophet, how N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; should come &#91;and&#93; strike the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> Declare you2f in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and publish in Migdol &#91;Tower&#93;, and publish in Nof &#91;presentability&#93; and in Tachpanches &#91;you will fill hands with pity&#93;: say you2f, Stand fast, and prepare youi; for the sword shall devour round about youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> Why are youri valiant &#91;men&#93; swept away? they stood not, because the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) did drive them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> He made many to fall, yes, one fell upon another: and they said, Arise, and let us go again to our own people, and to the land of our nativity, from the oppressing sword.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> They did cry there, Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; &#91;is but&#93; a noise; he has passed the time appointed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> &#91;As&#93; I live, says the King, whose name &#91;is&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, Surely as Tavor &#91;Mound&#93; &#91;is&#93; among the mountains, and as Carmel &#91;Garden-land&#93; by the sea, &#91;so&#93; shall he come.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> O youi daughter dwelling in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, furnish yourself to go into captivity: for Nof &#91;presentability&#93; shall be waste and desolate without an inhabitant.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; &#91;is like&#93; a very fair heifer, &#91;but&#93; destruction comes; it comes out of the north.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Also her hired men &#91;are&#93; in the midst of her like fatted bullocks; for they also are turned back, &#91;and&#93; are fled away together: they did not stand, because the day of their calamity was come upon them, &#91;and&#93; the time of their visitation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> The voice thereof shall go like a serpent; for they shall march with an army, and come against her with axes, as hewers of wood.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> They shall cut down her forest, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), though it cannot be searched; because they are more than the grasshoppers, and &#91;are&#93; innumerable.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> The daughter of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; shall be confounded; she shall be delivered into the hand of the people of the north.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, says; Behold, I will punish the multitude of No, and Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93;, and Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, with their gods, and their kings; even Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93;, and &#91;all&#93; them that trust in him:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> And I will deliver them into the hand of those that seek their lives, and into the hand of N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and into the hand of his servants: and afterward it shall be inhabited, as in the days of old, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> But fear not youi, O my servant Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;, and be not dismayed, O Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: for, behold, I will save youi from afar off, and youri seed from the land of their captivity; and Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93; shall return, and be in rest and at ease, and none shall make &#91;him&#93; afraid.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> Fear youi not, O Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93; my servant, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): for I &#91;am&#93; with youi; for I will make a full end of all the nations to which I have driven youi: but I will not make a full end of youi, but correct youi in measure; yet will I not leave youi wholly unpunished.</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> The word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) that came to Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; the prophet against the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;, before that Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; struck ‘Azah &#91;The Strong&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Behold, waters rise up out of the north, and shall be an overflowing flood, and shall overflow the land, and all that is in it; the city, and them that dwell in it: then the men shall cry, and all the inhabitants of the land shall howl.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> Because of the day that comes to spoil (plunder; prey; booty) all the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;, &#91;and&#93; to cut off from Tzor (Tyrus) &#91;a rock&#93; and Tzidon &#91;Hunting&#93; every helper that remains: for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) will spoil (plunder; prey; booty) the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;, the remnant of the country of Kaftor &#91;A Crown&#93;.</span>

<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> Baldness is come upon ‘Azah &#91;The Strong&#93;; Ashkelon &#91;The Fire of Infamy: I shall be Weighed&#93; is cut off &#91;with&#93; the remnant of their valley: how long will youi cut yourself?</span>

<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> Against Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Woe unto Nevo &#91;Prophet&#93;! for it is spoiled: Kiryatayim &#91;two cities&#93; is confounded &#91;and&#93; taken: Misgav &#91;refuge&#93; is confounded and dismayed.</span>

Also youi shall be cut down, O Madmen; the sword shall pursue youi.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> For in the going up of Luchit &#91;tablets (small, flat ornaments or jewelry)&#93; continual weeping shall go up; for in the going down of Horonayim &#91;two caves&#93; &#91;two caves&#93; the enemies have heard a cry of destruction.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> For because youi have trusted in youri works and in youri treasures, youi shall also be taken: and K’mosh &#91;Subduer&#93; shall go forth into captivity &#91;with&#93; his priests and his princes together.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> And the spoiler shall come upon every city, and no city shall escape: the valley also shall perish, and the plain shall be destroyed, as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has spoken.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; has been at ease from his youth, and he has settled on his lees (sediment settled in liquid; the dregs), and has not been emptied from vessel to vessel, neither has he gone into captivity: therefore his taste remained in him, and his scent is not changed.</span>

</div>

</div>

</div>

</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Make you2f him drunken: for he magnified &#91;himself&#93; against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; also shall wallow (to approve, commend, accept) in his vomit, and he also shall be in ridicule (laughing at enemies’ threats).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> O you2f that dwell in Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, leave the cities, and dwell in the rock, and be like the dove &#91;that&#93; makes her nest in the sides of the hole’s mouth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> We have heard the pride of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, (he is exceeding proud) his loftiness, and his arrogancy, and his pride, and the haughtiness of his heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> I know his wrath, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); but &#91;it shall&#93; not &#91;be&#93; so; his lies shall not so effect &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> Therefore will I howl for Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, and I will cry out for all Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;; &#91;my heart&#93; shall mourn for the men of Kir-Heres &#91;wall of potsherds&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> O vine of Sivnah &#91;Fragrance&#93;, I will weep for youi with the weeping of Ya’zer &#91;Helped&#93;: youri plants are gone over the sea, they reach &#91;even&#93; to the sea of Ya’zer &#91;Helped&#93;: the spoiler is fallen upon youri summer fruits and upon youri vintage.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And joy and gladness is taken from the plentiful field, and from the land of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;; and I have caused wine to fail from the winepresses: none shall trample with shouting; &#91;their&#93; shouting &#91;shall be&#93; no shouting.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> From the cry of Heshbon &#91;Stronghold&#93; &#91;even&#93; unto El’aleh &#91;God is Ascending&#93;, &#91;and even&#93; unto Yachatz &#91;Trampled Down&#93;, have they uttered their voice, from Tzo’ar &#91;Insignificance&#93; &#91;even&#93; unto Horonayim &#91;two caves&#93; &#91;two caves&#93;, &#91;as&#93; an heifer of three years old: for the waters also of Nimrim &#91;pure&#93; shall be desolate.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> Moreover I will cause to cease in Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), him that offers in the high places, and him that burns incense to his gods.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> Therefore my heart shall sound for Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; like pipes, and my heart shall sound like pipes for the men of Kir-Heres &#91;wall of potsherds&#93;: because the riches &#91;that&#93; he has gotten are perished.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> &#91;There shall be&#93; lamentation generally upon all the housetops of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, and in the streets thereof: for I have broken Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; like a vessel wherein &#91;is&#93; no pleasure, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> They shall howl, &#91;saying&#93;, How is it broken down! how has Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; turned the back with shame! so shall Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; be a ridicule (laughing at enemies’ threats) and a dismaying to all them about him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> For thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Behold, he shall fly as an eagle, and shall spread his wings over Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> K’riot &#91;Surrounded by a Wall&#93; is taken, and the strong holds are surprised, and the mighty men’s hearts in Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; at that day shall be as the heart of a woman in her pangs (extreme sharp pains).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> And Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; shall be destroyed from &#91;being&#93; a people, because he has magnified &#91;himself&#93; against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> Fear, and the pit, and the snare, &#91;shall be&#93; upon youi, O inhabitant of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> He that flees from the fear shall fall into the pit; and he that gets up out of the pit shall be taken in the snare: for I will bring upon it, &#91;even&#93; upon Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, the year of their visitation, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> Woe be unto youi, O Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;! the people of K’mosh &#91;Subduer&#93; perishes: for youri sons are taken captives, and youri daughters captives.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> Concerning the ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93;, thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Has Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; no sons? has he no heir? why &#91;then&#93; does their king inherit Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93;, and his people dwell in his cities?</span>

<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> For I have sworn by myself, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that Botzrah &#91;sheepfold or fortress&#93; shall become a desolation, a reproach (disgrace; shame), a waste, and a curse; and all the cities thereof shall be perpetual wastes.</span>

<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> Also Edom &#91;Red&#93; shall be a desolation: everyone that goes by it shall be astonished, and shall hiss at all the plagues thereof.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> As in the overthrow of S’dom &#91;Burning&#93; and ‘Amora &#91;Submersion&#93; and the neighbour &#91;cities&#93; thereof, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), no man shall abide there, neither shall a Son of man dwell in it.</span>

<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> Behold, he shall come up like a lion from the swelling of Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; against the habitation of the strong: but I will suddenly make him run away from her: and who &#91;is&#93; a chosen &#91;man, that&#93; I may appoint over her? for who &#91;is&#93; like me? and who will appoint me the time? and who &#91;is&#93; that shepherd that will stand before me?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Therefore hear the counsel of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that he has taken against Edom &#91;Red&#93;; and his purposes, that he has purposed against the inhabitants of Teman &#91;south&#93;: Surely the least of the flock shall draw them out: surely he shall make their habitations desolate with them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> The earth is moved at the noise of their fall, at the cry the noise thereof was heard in the Red sea.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Concerning Dammesek (Damascus) &#91;silent is the sackcloth weaver&#93;. Hamat &#91;Fortress&#93; is confounded, and Arpad &#91;I shall be spread out (or supported)&#93;: for they have heard evil tidings: they are fainthearted; &#91;there is&#93; sorrow on the sea; it cannot be quiet.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Dammesek (Damascus) &#91;silent is the sackcloth weaver&#93; is waxed feeble, &#91;and&#93; turns herself to flee, and fear has seized on &#91;her&#93;: anguish and sorrows have taken her, as a woman in travail.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> How is the city of praise not left, the city of my joy!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Therefore her young men shall fall in her streets, and all the men of war shall be cut off in that day, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And I will kindle a fire in the wall of Dammesek (Damascus) &#91;silent is the sackcloth weaver&#93;, and it shall consume the palaces of Ben-Hadad &#91;son of the (false god) Hadad&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> Concerning Kedar &#91;dark&#93;, and concerning the kingdoms of Hatzor &#91;Castle&#93;, which N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; shall strike, thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Arise you2f, go up to Kedar &#91;dark&#93;, and spoil (plunder; prey; booty) the men of the east.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Their tents and their flocks shall they take away: they shall take to themselves their curtains, and all their vessels, and their camels; and they shall cry unto them, Fear &#91;is&#93; on every side.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Flee, get you far off, dwell deep, O you2f inhabitants of Hatzor &#91;Castle&#93;, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); for N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; has taken counsel against you, and has conceived a purpose against you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> Arise, get you up unto the wealthy nation, that dwells without care, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), which have neither gates nor bars, &#91;which&#93; dwell alone.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And their camels shall be a booty, and the multitude of their cattle a spoil (plunder; prey; booty): and I will scatter into all winds them &#91;that are&#93; in the utmost corners; and I will bring their calamity from all sides thereof, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And Hatzor &#91;Castle&#93; shall be a dwelling for dragons, &#91;and&#93; a desolation forever: there shall no man abide there, nor &#91;any&#93; Son of man dwell in it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> The word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) that came to Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; the prophet against ‘Eilam &#91;eternity&#93; in the beginning of the reign of Tzidkiyah &#91;Yehovah is righteous&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;; Behold, I will break the bow of ‘Eilam &#91;eternity&#93;, the chief of their might.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And upon ‘Eilam &#91;eternity&#93; will I bring the four winds from the four quarters of heaven, and will scatter them toward all those winds; and there shall be no nation to which the outcasts of ‘Eilam &#91;eternity&#93; shall not come.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> For I will cause ‘Eilam &#91;eternity&#93; to be dismayed before their enemies, and before them that seek their life: and I will bring evil upon them, &#91;even&#93; my fierce anger, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); and I will send the sword after them, till I have consumed them:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> And I will set my throne in ‘Eilam &#91;eternity&#93;, and will destroy from there the king and the princes, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> But it shall come to pass in the latter days, &#91;that&#93; I will bring again the captivity of ‘Eilam &#91;eternity&#93;, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> The word that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke against Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; &#91;and&#93; against the land of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93; by Yirmeyahu &#91;whom Yehovah has appointed&#93; the prophet.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> Declare you2f among the nations, and publish, and set up a Flag (banner); publish, &#91;and&#93; conceal not: say, Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; is taken, Bel &#91;lord&#93; is shamed, M’rodakh &#91;your rebellion&#93; is broken in pieces; her idols are confounded, her images are broken in pieces.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> For out of the north there comes up a nation against her, which shall make her land desolate, and none shall dwell in it: they shall remove, they shall depart, both man and beast.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> In those days, and in that time, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; shall come, they and the children of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; together, going and weeping: they shall go, and seek the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> They shall ask the way to Tziyon &#91;parched place&#93; with their faces toward it, &#91;saying&#93;, Come, and let us join ourselves to the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in a perpetual covenant &#91;that&#93; shall not be forgotten.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> My people has been lost sheep: their shepherds have caused them to go astray, they have turned them away &#91;on&#93; the mountains: they have gone from mountain to hill, they have forgotten their resting place.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> All that found them have devoured them: and their adversaries said, We offend (make angry; make one stumble; violate) not, because they have sinned against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the habitation of justice, even the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the hope of their fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> Remove out of the midst of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and go forth out of the land of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;, and be as the he goats before the flocks.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> For, lo, I will raise and cause to come up against Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; an assembly of great nations from the north country: and they shall set themselves in array (to put on; to clothe) against her; from there she shall be taken: their arrows &#91;shall be&#93; as of a mighty expert man; none shall return in vain.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> And Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93; shall be a spoil (plunder; prey; booty): all that spoil (plunder; prey; booty) her shall be satisfied, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Amos 7:8; Amos 8:2; Zechariah 5:2"> Because you2f were glad, because you2f rejoiced, O you2f destroyers of my heritage, because you2f are grown fat as the heifer at grass, and bellow as bulls;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 12:28; Ezekiel 12:25; Ezekiel 12:22–23"> Your mother shall be sore confounded; she that bore you shall be ashamed: behold, the hindermost of the nations &#91;shall be&#93; a wilderness, a dry land, and a desert.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Ezekiel 11:3; Ezekiel 11:7; Zechariah 4:2"> Because of the wrath of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) it shall not be inhabited, but it shall be wholly desolate: everyone that goes by Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; shall be astonished, and hiss at all her plagues.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> Put yourselves in array (to put on; to clothe) against Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; round about: all you2f that bend the bow, shoot at her, spare no arrows: for she has sinned against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> Shout against her round about: she has given her hand: her foundations are fallen, her walls are thrown down: for it &#91;is&#93; the vengeance of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): take vengeance upon her; as she has done, do unto her.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> Cut off the sower from Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and him that handles the sickle in the time of harvest: for fear of the oppressing sword they shall turn everyone to his people, and they shall flee everyone to his own land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; &#91;is&#93; a scattered sheep; the lions have driven &#91;him&#93; away: first the king of Ashur &#91;the level plain&#93; (the land of the sons of Shem (name, renown (great reputation; being well-known))) has devoured him; and last this N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; has broken his bones.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> Therefore thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Behold, I will punish the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; and his land, as I have punished the king of Ashur &#91;the level plain&#93; (the land of the sons of Shem (name, renown (great reputation; being well-known))).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> And I will bring Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; again to his habitation, and he shall feed on Carmel &#91;Garden-land&#93; and Bashan &#91;Fruitful&#93;, and his soul shall be satisfied upon mount Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93; and Gil’ad &#91;Rocky Region&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> In those days, and in that time, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the iniquity of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; shall be sought for, and &#91;there shall be&#93; none; and the sins of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and they shall not be found: for I will pardon them whom I reserve.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Go up against the land of Meratayim &#91;double rebellion&#93;, &#91;even&#93; against it, and against the inhabitants of P’kod &#91;visitation&#93;: waste and utterly destroy after them, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and do according to all that I have commanded youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> A sound of battle &#91;is&#93; in the land, and of great destruction.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> How is the hammer of the whole earth cut asunder and broken! how is Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; become a desolation among the nations!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> I have laid a snare for youi, and youi are also taken, O Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and youi were not aware: youi are found, and also caught, because youi have striven against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has opened his armoury, and has brought forth the weapons of his indignation: for this &#91;is&#93; the work of the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93; in the land of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Come against her from the utmost border, open her storehouses: cast her up as heaps, and destroy her utterly: let nothing of her be left.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Slay all her bullocks; let them go down to the slaughter: woe unto them! for their day is come, the time of their visitation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> The voice of them that flee and escape out of the land of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, to declare in Tziyon &#91;parched place&#93; the vengeance of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, the vengeance of his temple.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Call to (to do entirely, do wholly) gether the archers against Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;: all you2f that bend the bow, camp against it round about; let none thereof escape: recompense her according to her work; according to all that she has done, do unto her: for she has been proud against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), against the Holy One of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Therefore shall her young men fall in the streets, and all her men of war shall be cut off in that day, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> Behold, I &#91;am&#93; against youi, &#91;O youi&#93; most proud, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;: for youi day is come, the time &#91;that&#93; I will visit youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And the most proud shall stumble and fall, and none shall raise him up: and I will kindle a fire in his cities, and it shall devour all round about him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;; The people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and the children of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; &#91;were&#93; oppressed together: and all that took them captives held them fast; they refused to let them go.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> Their Redeemer &#91;is&#93; strong; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93; &#91;is&#93; his name: he shall thoroughly plead their cause, that he may give rest to the land, and disquiet the inhabitants of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> A sword &#91;is&#93; upon the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and upon the inhabitants of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and upon her princes, and upon her wise &#91;men&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> A sword &#91;is&#93; upon the liars; and they shall dote: a sword &#91;is&#93; upon her mighty men; and they shall be dismayed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> A sword &#91;is&#93; upon their horses, and upon their chariots, and upon all the mingled people that &#91;are&#93; in the midst of her; and they shall become as women: a sword &#91;is&#93; upon her treasures; and they shall be robbed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> A drought &#91;is&#93; upon her waters; and they shall be dried up: for it &#91;is&#93; the land of graven images, and they are mad upon &#91;their&#93; idols.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> Therefore the wild beasts of the desert with the wild beasts of the islands shall dwell &#91;there&#93;, and the owls shall dwell in it: and it shall be no more inhabited forever; neither shall it be dwelt in from generation to generation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> As God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; overthrew S’dom &#91;Burning&#93; and ‘Amora &#91;Submersion&#93; and the neighbour &#91;cities&#93; thereof, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); &#91;so&#93; shall no man abide there, neither shall any Son of man dwell in it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> Behold, a people shall come from the north, and a great nation, and many kings shall be raised up from the coasts of the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> They shall hold the bow and the lance: they &#91;are&#93; cruel, and will not show mercy: their voice shall roar like the sea, and they shall ride upon horses, &#91;everyone&#93; put in array (to put on; to clothe), like a man to the battle, against youi, O daughter of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> The king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; has heard the report of them, and his hands waxed feeble: anguish took hold of him, &#91;and&#93; pangs (extreme sharp pains) as of a woman in travail.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> Behold, he shall come up like a lion from the swelling of Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; unto the habitation of the strong: but I will make them suddenly run away from her: and who &#91;is&#93; a chosen &#91;man, that&#93; I may appoint over her? for who &#91;is&#93; like me? and who will appoint me the time? and who &#91;is&#93; that shepherd that will stand before me?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> Therefore hear you2f the counsel of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that he has taken against Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;; and his purposes, that he has purposed against the land of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;: Surely the least of the flock shall draw them out: surely he shall make &#91;their&#93; habitation desolate with them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> At the noise of the taking of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; the earth is moved, and the cry is heard among the nations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:60; Jeremiah 11:21; 2 Chronicles 36:21"> Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Behold, I will raise up against Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and against them that dwell in the midst of them that rise up against me, a destroying wind;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="1 Kings 13:20; Jonah 1:1; Jeremiah 1:11"> And will send unto Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; fanners, that shall fan (a fork-shaped tool to throw grain in the air, to let he wind separate the wheat from the chaff) her, and shall empty her land: for in the day of trouble they shall be against her round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 39:2; 2 Chronicles 36:5–8; 2 Kings 23:34"> Against &#91;him that&#93; bends let the archer bend his bow, and against &#91;him that&#93; lifts himself up in his brigandine (armor): and spare you2f not her young men; destroy you2f utterly all her host.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:16; Ezekiel 1:3; Jeremiah 1:2"> Thus the slain shall fall in the land of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;, and &#91;they that are&#93; thrust through in her streets.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:1; Galatians 1:15–16"> For Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; &#91;has&#93; not &#91;been&#93; forsaken, nor Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; of his God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;; though their land was filled with sin against the Holy One of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 6:12; Exodus 4:10–16; 1 Kings 3:7–9"> Flee out of the midst of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and deliver every man his soul: be not cut off in her iniquity; for this &#91;is&#93; the time of the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; vengeance; he will render unto her a recompense.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mark 16:15–16; Ezekiel 3:27; Numbers 22:20"> Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; &#91;has been&#93; a golden cup in the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; hand, that made all the earth drunken: the nations have drunken of her wine; therefore the nations are mad.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Joshua 1:5; Joshua 1:9; Isaiah 51:7"> Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; is suddenly fallen and destroyed: howl for her; take balm (a medicine; rosin from a bush) for her pain, if so be she may be healed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Luke 21:15; Exodus 4:11–12; Luke 12:12"> We would have healed Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, but she is not healed: forsake her, and let us go everyone into his own country: for her judgment reaches unto heaven, and is lifted up &#91;even&#93; to the skies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:28; 2 Corinthians 10:4–5; Isaiah 44:26–28"> The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has brought forth our righteousness: come, and let us declare in Tziyon &#91;parched place&#93; the work of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And I will render unto Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; and to all the inhabitants of Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93; all their evil that they have done in Tziyon &#91;parched place&#93; in your sight, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Set you2f up a Flag (banner) in the land, blow the trumpet among the nations, prepare the nations against her, call to (to do entirely, do wholly) gether against her the kingdoms of Ararat &#91;the curse reversed&#93;, Minni &#91;division&#93;, and Ashchenaz &#91;scattered fire&#93;; appoint a captain against her; cause the horses to come up as the rough caterpillars.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> For thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; The daughter of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; &#91;is&#93; like a threshing floor, &#91;it is&#93; time to thresh her: yet a little while, and the time of her harvest shall come.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; has devoured me, he has crushed me, he has made me an empty vessel, he has swallow (to approve, commend, accept)ed me up like a dragon, he has filled his belly with my delicates, he has cast me out.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> My people, go you2f out of the midst of her, and deliver you2f every man his soul from the fierce anger of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50"> You2f that have escaped the sword, go away, stand not still: remember the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) afar off, and let Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; come into your mind.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v52" data-v="52"> For what reason, behold, the days come, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that I will do judgment upon her graven images: and through all her land the wounded shall groan.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v53" data-v="53"> Though Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; should mount up to heaven, and though she should fortify the height of her strength, &#91;yet&#93; from me shall spoilers come unto her, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>

</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Isaiah 41:25; Jeremiah 4:6; Jeremiah 10:22"> And all the army of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;, that &#91;were&#93; with the captain of the guard, broke down all the walls of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 43:10; Jeremiah 25:9; Jeremiah 9:11"> Then N’vuzar’adan &#91;Nebo has given seed&#93; the captain of the guard carried away captive &#91;certain&#93; of the poor of the people, and the residue (part that remains, the rest) of the people that remained in the city, and those that fell away, that fell to the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and the rest of the multitude.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Jeremiah 7:9; Jeremiah 19:4; Acts 7:41"> But N’vuzar’adan &#91;Nebo has given seed&#93; the captain of the guard left &#91;certain&#93; of the poor of the land for vinedressers and for husbandmen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:7–8; 1 Kings 18:46; 1 Peter 1:13"> Also the pillars of brass that &#91;were&#93; in the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and the bases, and the brazen sea that &#91;was&#93; in the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93; broke, and carried all the brass of them to Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 3:8–9; Jeremiah 15:20"> The caldrons also, and the shovels, and the snuffers, and the bowls, and the spoons, and all the vessels of brass which they ministered, took they away.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Jeremiah 1:8; Jeremiah 20:11"> And the basons, and the firepans, and the bowls, and the caldrons, and the candlesticks, and the spoons, and the cups; &#91;that&#93; which &#91;was&#93; of gold &#91;in&#93; gold, and &#91;that&#93; which &#91;was&#93; of silver &#91;in&#93; silver, took the captain of the guard away.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> The two pillars, one sea, and twelve brazen bulls that &#91;were&#93; under the bases, which king Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; had made in the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): the brass of all these vessels was without weight.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> And &#91;concerning&#93; the pillars, the height of one pillar &#91;was&#93; eighteen cubits; and a fillet (an ornamental band around the top of a pillar; a thin band or strip) of twelve cubits did compass (go around, encircle; encompass) it; and the thickness thereof &#91;was&#93; four fingers: &#91;it was&#93; hollow.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> And a column of brass &#91;was&#93; upon it; and the height of one column &#91;was&#93; five cubits, with network and pomegranates upon the columns round about, all &#91;of&#93; brass. The second pillar also and the pomegranates &#91;were&#93; like unto these.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And there were ninety and six pomegranates on a side; &#91;and&#93; all the pomegranates upon the network &#91;were&#93; an hundred round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And the captain of the guard took S’rayah &#91;Yehovah is ruler&#93; the chief priest, and Tzefan’yahu &#91;Yehovah has treasured&#93; the second priest, and the three keepers of the door:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> He took also out of the city an eunuch, which had the charge of the men of war; and seven men of them that were near the king’s person, which were found in the city; and the principal scribe of the host, who mustered the people of the land; and threescore men of the people of the land, that were found in the midst of the city.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> So N’vuzar’adan &#91;Nebo has given seed&#93; the captain of the guard took them, and brought them to the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; to Rivlah &#91;Fertility&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; struck them, and put them to death in Rivlah &#91;Fertility&#93; in the land of Hamat &#91;Fortress&#93;. Thus Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; was carried away captive out of his own land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> This &#91;is&#93; the people whom N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; carried away captive: in the seventh year three thousand Y’hudim (Judeans) and three and twenty:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> In the eighteenth year of N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; he carried away captive from Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; eight hundred thirty and two persons:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> In the three and twentieth year of N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; N’vuzar’adan &#91;Nebo has given seed&#93; the captain of the guard carried away captive of the Y’hudim (Judeans) seven hundred forty and five persons: all the persons &#91;were&#93; four thousand and six hundred.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And it came to pass in the seven and thirtieth year of the captivity of Y’hoyakhin &#91;Yehovah establishes&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, in the twelfth month, in the five and twentieth &#91;day&#93; of the month, &#91;that&#93; Eveel-M’rodakh &#91;man of bitter contrition&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; in the &#91;first&#93; year of his reign lifted up the head of Y’hoyakhin &#91;Yehovah establishes&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and brought him forth out of prison,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And spoke kindly unto him, and set his throne above the throne of the kings that &#91;were&#93; with him in Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And changed his prison garments: and he did continually eat bread before him all the days of his life.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And &#91;for&#93; his diet, there was a continual diet given him of the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, every day a portion until the day of his death, all the days of his life.</span>
</div>
</div>

</div>
